<file path="Converters/IsEqualConverter.cs">
using System.Globalization;
using System.Windows.Data;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Converters;

/// <summary>
/// يُرجع True لو القيمتان متساويتان — يُستخدم لتحديد الزر النشط في الـ Sidebar.
/// </summary>
public class IsEqualConverter : IMultiValueConverter
{
    public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        => values.Length == 2 && Equals(values[0], values[1]);

    public object[] ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        => throw new NotImplementedException();
}
</file>

<file path="Views/InputDialog.cs">
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Views;

/// <summary>
/// نافذة إدخال نص بسيطة — تُستخدم لطلب سبب الإلغاء وما شابهه.
/// تُعيد null إذا ضغط المستخدم إلغاء، أو النص المُدخل إذا أكّد.
/// </summary>
public static class InputDialog
{
    public static string? Show(string prompt, string title = "إدخال", Window? owner = null)
    {
        var dlg = new Window
        {
            Title = title,
            Width = 420,
            Height = 200,
            WindowStartupLocation = WindowStartupLocation.CenterOwner,
            Owner = owner,
            ResizeMode = ResizeMode.NoResize,
            FlowDirection = FlowDirection.RightToLeft,
            WindowStyle = WindowStyle.ToolWindow
        };

        var grid = new Grid { Margin = new Thickness(20) };
        grid.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition { Height = GridLength.Auto });
        grid.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition { Height = new GridLength(8) });
        grid.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition { Height = GridLength.Auto });
        grid.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition { Height = new GridLength(12) });
        grid.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition { Height = GridLength.Auto });

        var lbl = new TextBlock
        {
            Text = prompt,
            TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap,
            FontSize = 14
        };
        Grid.SetRow(lbl, 0);

        var txt = new TextBox
        {
            FontSize = 14,
            Padding = new Thickness(6),
            MaxLength = 200
        };
        Grid.SetRow(txt, 2);

        var btnPanel = new StackPanel
        {
            Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal,
            HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left
            
        };
        Grid.SetRow(btnPanel, 4);

        string? result = null;

        var btnOk = new Button
        {
            Content = "تأكيد",
            Width = 90,
            Height = 34,
            FontSize = 13,
            IsDefault = true
        };
        btnOk.Click += (_, _) =>
        {
            result = txt.Text;
            dlg.DialogResult = true;
        };

        var btnCancel = new Button
        {
            Content = "إلغاء",
            Width = 90,
            Height = 34,
            FontSize = 13,
            IsCancel = true
        };
        btnCancel.Click += (_, _) => dlg.DialogResult = false;
        btnCancel.Margin = new Thickness(10, 0, 0, 0);

        btnPanel.Children.Add(btnOk);
        btnPanel.Children.Add(btnCancel);

        grid.Children.Add(lbl);
        grid.Children.Add(txt);
        grid.Children.Add(btnPanel);

        dlg.Content = grid;
        dlg.Loaded += (_, _) => txt.Focus();

        var confirmed = dlg.ShowDialog();
        return confirmed == true ? result : null;
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Converters/BoolToVisibilityConverter.cs">
using System.Globalization;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Data;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Converters;

public class BoolToVisibilityConverter : IValueConverter
{
    public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        => value is true ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;

    public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        => value is Visibility.Visible;
}
</file>

<file path="Converters/DecimalToStringConverter.cs">
using System.Globalization;
using System.Windows.Data;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Converters;

public class DecimalToStringConverter : IValueConverter
{
    public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        => value is decimal d ? d.ToString("N2") : string.Empty;

    public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        => decimal.TryParse(value?.ToString(), out var result) ? result : 0m;
}
</file>

<file path="Converters/EnumDisplayConverter.cs">
// Converters/EnumDisplayConverter.cs
using System;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Windows.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Models;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Converters;

public class EnumDisplayConverter : IValueConverter
{
    public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
    {
        return value switch
        {
            // CustomerType
            CustomerType.Hospital => "مستشفى",
            CustomerType.Person => "فرد",

            // PaymentType
            PaymentType.Cash => "نقدي",
            PaymentType.Credit => "آجل",
            PaymentType.Partial => "جزئي",

            // SafeType
            SafeType.Cash => "خزينة نقدية",
            SafeType.Bank => "بنك",

            // BloodBagStatus
            BloodBagStatus.Available => "متاح",
            BloodBagStatus.Sold => "مباع",
            BloodBagStatus.Expired => "منتهي الصلاحية",
            BloodBagStatus.Damaged => "تالف",
            BloodBagStatus.Reserved => "محجوز",

            // VoucherType
            VoucherType.Receipt => "سند قبض",
            VoucherType.Payment => "سند صرف",

            // BloodGroup
            BloodGroup.APositive => "A+",
            BloodGroup.ANegative => "A-",
            BloodGroup.BPositive => "B+",
            BloodGroup.BNegative => "B-",
            BloodGroup.ABPositive => "AB+",
            BloodGroup.ABNegative => "AB-",
            BloodGroup.OPositive => "O+",
            BloodGroup.ONegative => "O-",

            _ => value?.ToString() ?? string.Empty
        };
    }

    public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        => throw new NotImplementedException();
}

<file path="Converters/ExpiringBagConverter.cs">
using System.Globalization;
using System.Windows.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Models;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Converters;

public class ExpiringBagConverter : IMultiValueConverter
{
    public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
    {
        if (values[0] is DateTime expiry && values[1] is BloodBagStatus status)
            return status == BloodBagStatus.Available && expiry <= DateTime.Now.AddDays(7);
        return false;
    }

    public object[] ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        => throw new NotImplementedException();
}
</file>

<file path="Converters/InverseBoolConverter.cs">
using System.Globalization;
using System.Windows.Data;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Converters;

public class InverseBoolConverter : IValueConverter
{
    public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        => value is bool b ? !b : value;

    public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        => value is bool b ? !b : value;
}
</file>

<file path="Converters/InverseBoolToVisibilityConverter.cs">
using System.Globalization;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Data;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Converters;

/// <summary>
/// يُحوّل bool → Visibility بشكل معكوس:
/// true  → Collapsed
/// false → Visible
/// مستخدم في TrialView لإخفاء زر "متابعة التجربة" عند انتهائها.
/// </summary>
public class InverseBoolToVisibilityConverter : IValueConverter
{
    public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        => value is true ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;

    public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        => throw new NotImplementedException();
}
</file>

using System.Globalization;
using System.Windows.Data;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Converters;

public class IsNegativeConverter : IValueConverter
{
    public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        => value is decimal d && d < 0;

    public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        => throw new NotImplementedException();
}

<file path="Converters/OverCreditLimitConverter.cs">
using System.Globalization;
using System.Windows.Data;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Converters;

public class OverCreditLimitConverter : IMultiValueConverter
{
    public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
    {
        if (values[0] is decimal balance && values[1] is decimal creditLimit)
            return creditLimit > 0 && balance > creditLimit;
        return false;
    }

    public object[] ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
        => throw new NotImplementedException();
}
</file>

<file path="Data/AppDbContext.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Models;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Configuration;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Data;

public class AppDbContext : DbContext
{
    public DbSet<Customer> Customers { get; set; }
    public DbSet<Safe> Safes { get; set; }
    public DbSet<BloodBag> BloodBags { get; set; }
    public DbSet<Invoice> Invoices { get; set; }
    public DbSet<InvoiceLine> InvoiceLines { get; set; }
    public DbSet<ReceiptVoucher> ReceiptVouchers { get; set; }
    public DbSet<JournalEntry> JournalEntries { get; set; }
    public DbSet<OrganizationSettings> OrgSettings { get; set; }   // ← جديد

    protected override void OnConfiguring(DbContextOptionsBuilder optionsBuilder)
    {
        var config = new ConfigurationBuilder()
            .SetBasePath(AppContext.BaseDirectory)
            .AddJsonFile("appsettings.json", optional: false, reloadOnChange: false)
            .Build();

        optionsBuilder.UseSqlServer(
            config.GetConnectionString("DefaultConnection"),
            sql => sql.CommandTimeout(60));
    }

    protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder mb)
    {
        // ─── Customer ────────────────────────────────────────
        mb.Entity<Customer>(e =>
        {
            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);
            e.Property(x => x.Name).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(200);
            e.Property(x => x.Phone).HasMaxLength(50);
            e.Property(x => x.Address).HasMaxLength(500);
            e.Property(x => x.Balance).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.Property(x => x.CreditLimit).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.Property(x => x.Type).HasConversion<string>();
            e.HasIndex(x => x.Name);
        });

        // ─── Safe ─────────────────────────────────────────────
        mb.Entity<Safe>(e =>
        {
            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);
            e.Property(x => x.Name).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(100);
            e.Property(x => x.Balance).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.Property(x => x.Type).HasConversion<string>();
        });

        // ─── BloodBag ─────────────────────────────────────────
        mb.Entity<BloodBag>(e =>
        {
            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);
            e.Property(x => x.BagNumber).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(50);
            e.HasIndex(x => x.BagNumber).IsUnique();
            e.Property(x => x.BloodGroup).HasConversion<string>();
            e.Property(x => x.Status).HasConversion<string>();
            e.Property(x => x.CostPrice).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.Property(x => x.SalePrice).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
        });

        // ─── Invoice ──────────────────────────────────────────
        mb.Entity<Invoice>(e =>
        {
            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);
            e.Property(x => x.InvoiceNumber).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(20);
            e.HasIndex(x => x.InvoiceNumber).IsUnique();
            e.Property(x => x.PaymentType).HasConversion<string>();
            e.Property(x => x.SubTotal).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.Property(x => x.DiscountAmount).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.Property(x => x.TotalAmount).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.Property(x => x.PaidAmount).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");

            e.HasOne(x => x.Customer)
             .WithMany(x => x.Invoices)
             .HasForeignKey(x => x.CustomerId)
             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            e.HasOne(x => x.Safe)
             .WithMany(x => x.Invoices)
             .HasForeignKey(x => x.SafeId)
             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);
        });

        // ─── InvoiceLine ──────────────────────────────────────
        mb.Entity<InvoiceLine>(e =>
        {
            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);
            e.Property(x => x.ProductName).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(300);
            e.Property(x => x.UnitPrice).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.Property(x => x.Total).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");

            e.HasOne(x => x.Invoice)
             .WithMany(x => x.Lines)
             .HasForeignKey(x => x.InvoiceId)
             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            e.HasOne(x => x.BloodBag)
             .WithMany(x => x.InvoiceLines)
             .HasForeignKey(x => x.BloodBagId)
             .IsRequired(false)
             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);
        });

        // ─── ReceiptVoucher ───────────────────────────────────
        mb.Entity<ReceiptVoucher>(e =>
        {
            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);
            e.Property(x => x.VoucherNumber).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(20);
            e.HasIndex(x => x.VoucherNumber).IsUnique();
            e.Property(x => x.Type).HasConversion<string>();
            e.Property(x => x.Amount).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.Property(x => x.PayeeName).HasMaxLength(200);

            e.HasOne(x => x.Safe)
             .WithMany(x => x.ReceiptVouchers)
             .HasForeignKey(x => x.SafeId)
             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            e.HasOne(x => x.Customer)
             .WithMany(x => x.ReceiptVouchers)
             .HasForeignKey(x => x.CustomerId)
             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            e.HasOne(x => x.RelatedInvoice)
             .WithMany(x => x.ReceiptVouchers)
             .HasForeignKey(x => x.RelatedInvoiceId)
             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);
        });

        // ─── JournalEntry ─────────────────────────────────────
        mb.Entity<JournalEntry>(e =>
        {
            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);
            e.Property(x => x.AccountCode).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(20);
            e.Property(x => x.AccountName).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(100);
            e.Property(x => x.RefType).HasMaxLength(50);
            e.Property(x => x.DebitAmount).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.Property(x => x.CreditAmount).HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)");
            e.HasIndex(x => new { x.RefType, x.RefId });

            e.HasOne(x => x.Invoice)
             .WithMany(x => x.JournalEntries)
             .HasForeignKey(x => x.InvoiceId)
             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            e.HasOne(x => x.ReceiptVoucher)
             .WithMany(x => x.JournalEntries)
             .HasForeignKey(x => x.ReceiptVoucherId)
             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);
        });

        // ─── OrganizationSettings ─────────────────────────────
        mb.Entity<OrganizationSettings>(e =>
        {
            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);
            e.Property(x => x.BankName).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(200);
            e.Property(x => x.SubTitle).HasMaxLength(200);
            e.Property(x => x.Address).HasMaxLength(500);
            e.Property(x => x.Phone).HasMaxLength(100);
            e.Property(x => x.LogoPath).HasMaxLength(500);
            e.Property(x => x.OwnerPinHash).HasMaxLength(64);
        });

        // ─── Seed ─────────────────────────────────────────────
        mb.Entity<Safe>().HasData(
            new Safe { Id = 1, Name = "الخزينة الرئيسية", Type = SafeType.Cash, Balance = 0, IsMain = true }
        );

        mb.Entity<OrganizationSettings>().HasData(
            new OrganizationSettings
            {
                Id = 1,
                BankName = "بنك الدم",
                SubTitle = "إدارة المحاسبة والمستودعات",
                Address = string.Empty,
                Phone = string.Empty
            }
        );
    }
}

<file path="Data/AutoBackupService.cs">
using System.IO;
using System.Text.Json;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Data;

/// <summary>
/// خدمة النسخ الاحتياطي اليومي التلقائي.
/// تعمل في الخلفية بدون أي تدخل من المستخدم.
/// </summary>
public sealed class AutoBackupService : IDisposable
{
    // ─── Singleton ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    public static readonly AutoBackupService Instance = new();

    // ─── Events ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    /// <summary>يُطلَق بعد كل نسخة احتياطية ناجحة بمسار الملف الناتج.</summary>
    public event Action<string>? BackupSucceeded;

    /// <summary>يُطلَق لو فشل الـ Backup بسبب خطأ.</summary>
    public event Action<Exception>? BackupFailed;

    /// <summary>آخر حالة للعرض في الـ UI (يُحدَّث من Background Thread).</summary>
    public string LastStatusText { get; private set; } = "جاري التحقق...";

    // ─── Internals ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private Timer? _timer;
    private bool _isRunning;

    // ملف JSON صغير يخزّن تاريخ آخر نسخة احتياطية
    private static readonly string StateFile = Path.Combine(
        Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.ApplicationData),
        "BloodBankAccounting",
        "backup_state.json");

    private AutoBackupService() { }

    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // Start — يُستدعى مرة واحدة من App.xaml.cs بعد فتح الـ MainWindow
    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    public void Start()
    {
        // فحص فوري عند الإطلاق
        Task.Run(CheckAndBackupAsync);

        // ثم كل ساعة (يضمن اصطياد منتصف الليل لو التطبيق ظل شغّالاً)
        _timer = new Timer(
            callback: _ => Task.Run(CheckAndBackupAsync),
            state: null,
            dueTime: TimeSpan.FromHours(1),
            period: TimeSpan.FromHours(1));
    }

    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // الـ Logic الأساسي
    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    private async Task CheckAndBackupAsync()
    {
        // منع التشغيل المتزامن لو الساعة دقت وهو لسه بيشتغل
        if (_isRunning) return;
        _isRunning = true;

        try
        {
            var lastDate = LoadLastBackupDate();

            // لو عملنا نسخة احتياطية النهارده قبل كده → مفيش حاجة
            if (lastDate.Date == DateTime.Today)
            {
                LastStatusText = $"آخر نسخة: {lastDate:hh:mm tt}";
                return;
            }

            LastStatusText = "جاري النسخ الاحتياطي...";

            var path = await DatabaseAdminService.BackupDatabaseAsync();

            // احفظ التاريخ بعد النجاح
            SaveLastBackupDate(DateTime.Now);
            LastStatusText = $"آخر نسخة: {DateTime.Now:hh:mm tt}";

            // احذف النسخ الأقدم من 30 يوم تلقائياً
            CleanOldBackups(daysToKeep: 30);

            BackupSucceeded?.Invoke(path);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            LastStatusText = "⚠ فشل النسخ الاحتياطي";
            BackupFailed?.Invoke(ex);
        }
        finally
        {
            _isRunning = false;
        }
    }

    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // State persistence
    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    private static DateTime LoadLastBackupDate()
    {
        try
        {
            if (!File.Exists(StateFile)) return DateTime.MinValue;
            var json = File.ReadAllText(StateFile);
            var state = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<BackupState>(json);
            return state?.LastBackupDate ?? DateTime.MinValue;
        }
        catch { return DateTime.MinValue; }
    }

    private static void SaveLastBackupDate(DateTime date)
    {
        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(StateFile)!);
        var json = JsonSerializer.Serialize(new BackupState { LastBackupDate = date });
        File.WriteAllText(StateFile, json);
    }

    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // حذف النسخ القديمة
    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    private static void CleanOldBackups(int daysToKeep)
    {
        try
        {
            var backupDir = Path.Combine(
                Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.MyDocuments),
                "BloodBankAccounting", "Backups");

            if (!Directory.Exists(backupDir)) return;

            var cutoff = DateTime.Today.AddDays(-daysToKeep);

            foreach (var file in Directory.GetFiles(backupDir, "*.bak"))
            {
                if (File.GetCreationTime(file) < cutoff)
                    File.Delete(file);
            }
        }
        catch { /* صامت — حذف النسخ القديمة مش حاجة حرجة */ }
    }

    public void Dispose() => _timer?.Dispose();

    private sealed class BackupState
    {
        public DateTime LastBackupDate { get; set; }
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Data/DatabaseAdminService.cs">
using Microsoft.Data.SqlClient;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Configuration;
using System.IO;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Data;

/// <summary>
/// عمليات الصيانة السرية لقاعدة البيانات — للمبرمج فقط.
/// </summary>
public static class DatabaseAdminService
{
    // ─── Connection string helper ─────────────────────────────────────────
    private static string GetConnectionString()
    {
        var config = new ConfigurationBuilder()
            .SetBasePath(AppContext.BaseDirectory)
            .AddJsonFile("appsettings.json", optional: false)
            .Build();
        return config.GetConnectionString("DefaultConnection")!;
    }

    private static string GetMasterConnectionString()
    {
        // نفس الـ connection string بس Database = master
        var builder = new SqlConnectionStringBuilder(GetConnectionString());
        builder.InitialCatalog = "master";
        return builder.ToString();
    }

    private static string GetDatabaseName()
    {
        var builder = new SqlConnectionStringBuilder(GetConnectionString());
        return builder.InitialCatalog;
    }

    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // 1. حذف قاعدة البيانات بالكامل
    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    /// <summary>
    /// يحذف قاعدة البيانات من الـ SQL Server نهائياً.
    /// يستخدم بعد الانتهاء من التجريب عند العميل.
    /// </summary>
    public static async Task DropDatabaseAsync()
    {
        var dbName = GetDatabaseName();

        await using var conn = new SqlConnection(GetMasterConnectionString());
        await conn.OpenAsync();

        // أوّلاً: فصل كل الـ connections الحالية
        var closeSessions = $"""
            ALTER DATABASE [{dbName}] SET SINGLE_USER WITH ROLLBACK IMMEDIATE;
            """;

        var dropDb = $"""
            DROP DATABASE IF EXISTS [{dbName}];
            """;

        await using (var cmd = new SqlCommand(closeSessions, conn))
            await cmd.ExecuteNonQueryAsync();

        await using (var cmd = new SqlCommand(dropDb, conn))
            await cmd.ExecuteNonQueryAsync();
    }

    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // 2. مسح كل البيانات مع الاحتفاظ بالهيكل والـ Seed
    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    /// <summary>
    /// يحذف كل الصفوف من كل الجداول ويُعيد تشغيل الـ Seed الأساسي
    /// (الخزينة الرئيسية + إعدادات المنظمة).
    /// </summary>
    public static async Task ClearAllDataAsync()
    {
        await using var conn = new SqlConnection(GetConnectionString());
        await conn.OpenAsync();

        // الترتيب مهم — نبدأ من الجداول اللي فيها FK تجاه غيرها
        string[] tables =
        [
            "JournalEntries",
            "InvoiceLines",
            "ReceiptVouchers",
            "Invoices",
            "BloodBags",
            "Customers",
        ];

        foreach (var table in tables)
        {
            var sql = $"DELETE FROM [{table}]; DBCC CHECKIDENT ('[{table}]', RESEED, 0);";
            await using var cmd = new SqlCommand(sql, conn);
            await cmd.ExecuteNonQueryAsync();
        }

        // إعادة ضبط رصيد الخزينة الرئيسية
        await using var resetSafe = new SqlCommand(
            "UPDATE Safes SET Balance = 0 WHERE IsMain = 1;", conn);
        await resetSafe.ExecuteNonQueryAsync();
    }

    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // 3. Backup قاعدة البيانات
    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    /// <summary>
    /// يعمل BACKUP DATABASE وبيحفظ الملف في مجلد Documents الخاص بالمستخدم.
    /// يرجع المسار الكامل لملف الـ .bak اللي اتنشأ.
    /// </summary>
    public static async Task<string> BackupDatabaseAsync()
    {
        var dbName = GetDatabaseName();
        var timestamp = DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd_HH-mm-ss");
        var fileName = $"{dbName}_Backup_{timestamp}.bak";

        // ─── أفضل مكان للـ backup ──────────────────────────────────────────
        // نستخدم مجلد مؤقت على سيرفر الـ SQL أوّل (SQL Server لازم يكون له
        // صلاحية الكتابة فيه)، وبعدين ننقل الملف لـ Documents.
        var sqlServerBackupDir = await GetSqlServerDefaultBackupPathAsync();
        var tempPath = Path.Combine(sqlServerBackupDir, fileName);

        // تنفيذ الـ BACKUP
        await using var conn = new SqlConnection(GetConnectionString());
        await conn.OpenAsync();

        var backupSql = $"""
            BACKUP DATABASE [{dbName}]
            TO DISK = N'{tempPath}'
            WITH FORMAT, INIT,
                 NAME = N'{dbName} Full Backup',
                 COMPRESSION,
                 STATS = 10;
            """;

        await using (var cmd = new SqlCommand(backupSql, conn) { CommandTimeout = 120 })
            await cmd.ExecuteNonQueryAsync();

        // ─── ننقل الملف لـ Documents\BloodBankAccounting\Backups ───────────
        var docsFolder = Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.MyDocuments);
        var destDir = Path.Combine(docsFolder, "BloodBankAccounting", "Backups");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(destDir);

        var destPath = Path.Combine(destDir, fileName);

        // لو الـ temp path مختلف عن الـ dest path ننقله
        if (!string.Equals(tempPath, destPath, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            File.Move(tempPath, destPath, overwrite: true);
        }

        return destPath;
    }

    // ─── helper: جيب مسار الـ Backup الافتراضي لـ SQL Server ──────────────
    private static async Task<string> GetSqlServerDefaultBackupPathAsync()
    {
        await using var conn = new SqlConnection(GetMasterConnectionString());
        await conn.OpenAsync();

        // نقرأ من الـ Registry عبر SQL Server
        var sql = """
            DECLARE @path NVARCHAR(512);
            EXEC master.dbo.xp_instance_regread
                N'HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE',
                N'Software\Microsoft\MSSQLServer\MSSQLServer',
                N'BackupDirectory',
                @path OUTPUT;
            SELECT @path;
            """;

        await using var cmd = new SqlCommand(sql, conn);
        var result = await cmd.ExecuteScalarAsync();

        if (result is string path && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(path))
            return path;

        // fallback لو فشل
        return Path.GetTempPath();
    }
}

-- Customers
IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sysobjects WHERE name='Customers')
CREATE TABLE Customers (
    Id           INT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
    Name         NVARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    Type         NVARCHAR(50)  NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Hospital',
    Phone        NVARCHAR(50)  NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    Address      NVARCHAR(500) NULL,
    Balance      DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    CreditLimit  DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    IsActive     BIT           NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    CreatedAt    DATETIME2     NOT NULL DEFAULT GETDATE()
);

-- Safes
IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sysobjects WHERE name='Safes')
CREATE TABLE Safes (
    Id      INT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
    Name    NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    Type    NVARCHAR(50)  NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Cash',
    Balance DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    IsMain  BIT           NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);

INSERT INTO Safes (Name, Type, Balance, IsMain)
SELECT 'الخزينة الرئيسية', 'Cash', 0, 1
WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM Safes WHERE IsMain = 1);

-- BloodBags
IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sysobjects WHERE name='BloodBags')
CREATE TABLE BloodBags (
    Id         INT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
    BagNumber  NVARCHAR(50)  NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    BloodGroup NVARCHAR(50)  NOT NULL,
    CostPrice  DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    SalePrice  DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    ExpiryDate DATETIME2     NOT NULL,
    EntryDate  DATETIME2     NOT NULL DEFAULT GETDATE(),
    Status     NVARCHAR(50)  NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Available',
    Notes      NVARCHAR(MAX) NULL
);

-- Invoices
IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sysobjects WHERE name='Invoices')
CREATE TABLE Invoices (
    Id             INT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
    InvoiceNumber  NVARCHAR(20)  NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    Date           DATETIME2     NOT NULL DEFAULT GETDATE(),
    PaymentType    NVARCHAR(20)  NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Cash',
    CustomerId     INT           NOT NULL REFERENCES Customers(Id),
    SafeId         INT           NULL     REFERENCES Safes(Id),
    SubTotal       DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    DiscountAmount DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    TotalAmount    DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    PaidAmount     DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    IsCancelled    BIT           NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    CancelReason   NVARCHAR(500) NULL,
    Notes          NVARCHAR(MAX) NULL,
    CreatedAt      DATETIME2     NOT NULL DEFAULT GETDATE()
);

-- InvoiceLines
IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sysobjects WHERE name='InvoiceLines')
CREATE TABLE InvoiceLines (

    InvoiceId  INT           NOT NULL REFERENCES Invoices(Id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    BloodBagId INT           NULL REFERENCES BloodBags(Id),
    UnitPrice  DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL,
    Quantity   INT           NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    Total      DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL
);

-- ReceiptVouchers
IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sysobjects WHERE name='ReceiptVouchers')
CREATE TABLE ReceiptVouchers (

    VoucherNumber    NVARCHAR(20)  NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    Type             NVARCHAR(20)  NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Receipt',
    Date             DATETIME2     NOT NULL DEFAULT GETDATE(),
    SafeId           INT           NOT NULL REFERENCES Safes(Id),
    CustomerId       INT           NULL     REFERENCES Customers(Id),
    RelatedInvoiceId INT           NULL     REFERENCES Invoices(Id),
    Amount           DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL,
    Notes            NVARCHAR(MAX) NULL,
    CreatedAt        DATETIME2     NOT NULL DEFAULT GETDATE()
);

-- JournalEntries
IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sysobjects WHERE name='JournalEntries')
CREATE TABLE JournalEntries (
    Id               INT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY,
    Date             DATETIME2     NOT NULL,
    AccountCode      NVARCHAR(20)  NOT NULL,
    AccountName      NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    DebitAmount      DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    CreditAmount     DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    RefType          NVARCHAR(50)  NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    RefId            INT           NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    InvoiceId        INT           NULL REFERENCES Invoices(Id),
    ReceiptVoucherId INT           NULL REFERENCES ReceiptVouchers(Id),
    Notes            NVARCHAR(MAX) NULL,
    CreatedAt        DATETIME2     NOT NULL DEFAULT GETDATE()
);

IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sys.indexes 
               WHERE name = 'IX_JournalEntries_Ref' 
               AND object_id = OBJECT_ID('JournalEntries'))
    CREATE INDEX IX_JournalEntries_Ref ON JournalEntries(RefType, RefId);
PRINT '✓ Database setup complete';

<file path="Helpers/ArabicNumberHelper.cs">
namespace BloodBankAccounting.Helpers;

/// <summary>
/// تحويل الأرقام إلى كلمات عربية لطباعة سندات القبض والدفع.
/// يدعم حتى 999,999,999.99
/// </summary>
public static class ArabicNumberHelper
{
    private static readonly string[] Units =
    [
        "", "واحد", "اثنان", "ثلاثة", "أربعة", "خمسة",
        "ستة", "سبعة", "ثمانية", "تسعة", "عشرة",
        "أحد عشر", "اثنا عشر", "ثلاثة عشر", "أربعة عشر", "خمسة عشر",
        "ستة عشر", "سبعة عشر", "ثمانية عشر", "تسعة عشر"
    ];

    private static readonly string[] Tens =
    [
        "", "عشرة", "عشرون", "ثلاثون", "أربعون", "خمسون",
        "ستون", "سبعون", "ثمانون", "تسعون"
    ];

    private static readonly string[] Hundreds =
    [
        "", "مائة", "مئتان", "ثلاثمائة", "أربعمائة", "خمسمائة",
        "ستمائة", "سبعمائة", "ثمانمائة", "تسعمائة"
    ];

    /// <summary>
    /// مثال: 1350.50 → "ألف وثلاثمائة وخمسون جنيهًا وخمسون قرشًا فقط لا غير"
    /// </summary>
    public static string ToWords(decimal amount)
    {
        if (amount == 0) return "صفر جنيه فقط لا غير";

        var whole = (long)Math.Truncate(amount);
        var fraction = (int)Math.Round((amount - whole) * 100);

        var result = ConvertWholeNumber(whole) + " جنيه";
        if (fraction > 0)
            result += $" و{ConvertWholeNumber(fraction)} قرش";

        return result + " فقط لا غير";
    }

    private static string ConvertWholeNumber(long n)
    {
        if (n <= 0) return "صفر";

        var parts = new List<string>();

        if (n >= 1_000_000)
        {
            var m = n / 1_000_000;
            n %= 1_000_000;
            parts.Add(ConvertChunk((int)m) + (m == 1 ? " مليون" : m == 2 ? " مليونان" : " مليون"));
        }

        if (n >= 1_000)
        {
            var t = n / 1_000;
            n %= 1_000;
            if (t == 1) parts.Add("ألف");
            else if (t == 2) parts.Add("ألفان");
            else if (t <= 10) parts.Add(ConvertChunk((int)t) + " آلاف");
            else parts.Add(ConvertChunk((int)t) + " ألف");
        }

        if (n > 0) parts.Add(ConvertChunk((int)n));

        return string.Join(" و", parts);
    }

    private static string ConvertChunk(int n)
    {
        if (n == 0) return "";

        var parts = new List<string>();

        int h = n / 100;
        if (h > 0) { parts.Add(Hundreds[h]); n %= 100; }

        if (n >= 20)
        {
            int t = n / 10;
            int u = n % 10;
            parts.Add(u > 0 ? Units[u] + " و" + Tens[t] : Tens[t]);
        }
        else if (n > 0)
        {
            parts.Add(Units[n]);
        }

        return string.Join(" و", parts);
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Helpers/JournalService.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Models;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Helpers;

public static class AccountCodes
{
    public const string Cash             = "1001";   // الخزينة النقدية
    public const string Bank             = "1002";   // البنك
    public const string AccountsRec      = "1100";   // ذمم مدينة (آجل)
    public const string BloodInventory   = "1200"; // مخزون الدم
    public const string Sales            = "4001";   // إيرادات المبيعات
    public const string COGS             = "5001";   // تكلفة المبيعات
    public const string Expenses         = "6001";   // المصاريف العمومية
}

public static class JournalService
{
    // ─── فاتورة نقدي ─────────────────────────────────────────
    public static void PostInvoice(AppDbContext db, Invoice invoice,
        string safeAccountCode = AccountCodes.Cash)
    {
        var entries = new List<JournalEntry>();
        var date = invoice.Date;
        var refId = invoice.Id;

        if (invoice.PaymentType == PaymentType.Cash)
        {
            entries.Add(Debit(date, safeAccountCode, "الخزينة", invoice.TotalAmount, "Invoice", refId, invoice.Id));
            entries.Add(Credit(date, AccountCodes.Sales, "إيرادات المبيعات", invoice.TotalAmount, "Invoice", refId, invoice.Id));
        }
        else
        {
            entries.Add(Debit(date, AccountCodes.AccountsRec, $"ذمم - {invoice.Customer?.Name}", invoice.TotalAmount, "Invoice", refId, invoice.Id));
            entries.Add(Credit(date, AccountCodes.Sales, "إيرادات المبيعات", invoice.TotalAmount, "Invoice", refId, invoice.Id));
        }

        db.JournalEntries.AddRange(entries);
    }

    // ─── فاتورة جزئي ─────────────────────────────────────────
    public static void PostPartialInvoice(AppDbContext db, Invoice invoice,
        decimal initialPayment, string safeAccountCode = AccountCodes.Cash)
    {
        var remaining = invoice.TotalAmount - initialPayment;
        var date = invoice.Date;
        var refId = invoice.Id;
        var name = invoice.Customer?.Name ?? "";
        var entries = new List<JournalEntry>();

        if (initialPayment > 0)
            entries.Add(Debit(date, safeAccountCode, "الخزينة",
                              initialPayment, "Invoice", refId, invoice.Id));

        if (remaining > 0)
            entries.Add(Debit(date, AccountCodes.AccountsRec, $"ذمم - {name}",
                              remaining, "Invoice", refId, invoice.Id));

        entries.Add(Credit(date, AccountCodes.Sales, "إيرادات المبيعات",
                           invoice.TotalAmount, "Invoice", refId, invoice.Id));

        db.JournalEntries.AddRange(entries);
    }

    // ─── سند قبض ─────────────────────────────────────────────
    public static void PostReceipt(AppDbContext db, ReceiptVoucher voucher,
        string safeAccountCode = AccountCodes.Cash)
    {
        var entries = new[]
        {
            Debit (voucher.Date, safeAccountCode,          "الخزينة",                        voucher.Amount, "Receipt", voucher.Id, receiptId: voucher.Id),
            Credit(voucher.Date, AccountCodes.AccountsRec, $"ذمم - {voucher.Customer?.Name}", voucher.Amount, "Receipt", voucher.Id, receiptId: voucher.Id)
        };
        db.JournalEntries.AddRange(entries);
    }

    // ─── سند دفع ─────────────────────────────────────────────
    /// <summary>
    /// قيد سند الدفع: مصاريف (مدين) ← خزينة (دائن)
    /// المال يخرج من الخزينة لصالح المستلم.
    /// </summary>
    public static void PostPayment(AppDbContext db, ReceiptVoucher voucher,
        string safeAccountCode = AccountCodes.Cash)
    {
        var payee = voucher.PayeeName ?? "—";
        var entries = new[]
        {
            Debit (voucher.Date, AccountCodes.Expenses, $"مصاريف - {payee}", voucher.Amount, "Payment", voucher.Id, receiptId: voucher.Id),
            Credit(voucher.Date, safeAccountCode,       "الخزينة",           voucher.Amount, "Payment", voucher.Id, receiptId: voucher.Id)
        };
        db.JournalEntries.AddRange(entries);
    }

    // ─── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    private static JournalEntry Debit(DateTime date, string code, string name, decimal amount,
        string refType, int refId, int? invoiceId = null, int? receiptId = null)
        => new()
        {
            Date = date,
            AccountCode = code,
            AccountName = name,
            DebitAmount = amount,
            CreditAmount = 0,
            RefType = refType,
            RefId = refId,
            InvoiceId = invoiceId,
            ReceiptVoucherId = receiptId,
            CreatedAt = DateTime.Now
        };

    private static JournalEntry Credit(DateTime date, string code, string name, decimal amount,
        string refType, int refId, int? invoiceId = null, int? receiptId = null)
        => new()
        {
            Date = date,
            AccountCode = code,
            AccountName = name,
            DebitAmount = 0,
            CreditAmount = amount,
            RefType = refType,
            RefId = refId,
            InvoiceId = invoiceId,
            ReceiptVoucherId = receiptId,
            CreatedAt = DateTime.Now
        };
}
</file>

<file path="Helpers/NumberGenerator.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Models;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Helpers;

public static class NumberGenerator
{
    public static async Task<string> NextInvoiceNumberAsync(AppDbContext db)
    {
        var year = DateTime.Now.Year;
        var count = await db.Invoices.CountAsync(x => x.CreatedAt.Year == year);
        return $"INV-{year}-{(count + 1):D5}";
    }

    /// <summary>??? ???</summary>
    public static async Task<string> NextVoucherNumberAsync(AppDbContext db)
    {
        var year = DateTime.Now.Year;
        var count = await db.ReceiptVouchers
            .CountAsync(x => x.Type == VoucherType.Receipt && x.CreatedAt.Year == year);
        return $"RCV-{year}-{(count + 1):D5}";
    }

    /// <summary>??? ???</summary>
    public static async Task<string> NextPaymentVoucherNumberAsync(AppDbContext db)
    {
        var year = DateTime.Now.Year;
        var count = await db.ReceiptVouchers
            .CountAsync(x => x.Type == VoucherType.Payment && x.CreatedAt.Year == year);
        return $"PAY-{year}-{(count + 1):D5}";
    }
}

<file path="Helpers/PinHelper.cs">
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Helpers;

public static class PinHelper
{
    /// <summary>تحويل الرمز السري إلى hash بـ SHA-256</summary>
    public static string Hash(string pin)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(pin)) return string.Empty;
        var bytes = SHA256.HashData(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(pin.Trim()));
        return Convert.ToHexString(bytes).ToLowerInvariant();
    }

    /// <summary>التحقق من صحة الرمز المُدخَل</summary>
    public static bool Verify(string pin, string storedHash)
        => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(storedHash) && Hash(pin) == storedHash;
}
</file>

<file path="Helpers/TrialManager.cs">
using Microsoft.Win32;
using System.IO;
using System.Management;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Helpers;

/// <summary>
/// نظام التجربة والتفعيل — حماية البرنامج من النسخ غير المرخّصة
/// </summary>
public static class TrialManager
{
    private const int TRIAL_DAYS = 20;
    // ⚠️ يجب أن يطابق تماماً قيمة SECRET في KeyGeneratorTool.csx
    private const string SECRET = "SH3RY4N_BL00D_B4NK_2025_S3CR3T!";

    // مسار الريجستري — تحت CurrentUser لتجنّب الحاجة لأذونات المسؤول
    private const string REG_PATH = @"SOFTWARE\Classes\Applications\BloodBankApp\Config";
    private const string VAL_INSTALL = "Cfg1";
    private const string VAL_LICENSE = "Cfg2";

    // ── Public API ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    /// <summary>
    /// يُرجع معرّف الجهاز الفريد بصيغة XXXX-XXXX-XXXX
    /// مبني على CPU ID + رقم تسلسل قرص النظام
    /// </summary>
    public static string GetMachineId()
    {
        var raw = $"{GetCpuId()}|{GetVolumeSerial()}";

        using var sha = SHA256.Create();
        var hash = sha.ComputeHash(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(raw));
        var hex = BitConverter.ToString(hash, 0, 6).Replace("-", "").ToUpper();

        return $"{hex[..4]}-{hex[4..8]}-{hex[8..12]}";
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// الأيام المتبقية من فترة التجربة.
    /// يُرجع int.MaxValue إذا كان البرنامج مفعّلاً.
    /// </summary>
    public static int DaysRemaining()
    {
        if (IsActivated()) return int.MaxValue;

        var installDate = GetOrSetInstallDate();
        var elapsed = (int)(DateTime.Now - installDate).TotalDays;
        return Math.Max(0, TRIAL_DAYS - elapsed);
    }

    /// <summary>هل يحق للبرنامج أن يعمل؟ (مفعّل أو التجربة لم تنته)</summary>
    public static bool CanRun() => IsActivated() || DaysRemaining() > 0;

    /// <summary>هل البرنامج مفعّل بكود صحيح مسجّل على هذا الجهاز؟</summary>
    public static bool IsActivated()
    {
        var stored = ReadReg(VAL_LICENSE);
        return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(stored)
            && stored == ComputeKey(GetMachineId());
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// محاولة تفعيل البرنامج بالكود المُدخَل.
    /// يُرجع true عند النجاح ويحفظ الترخيص في الريجستري.
    /// </summary>
    public static bool Activate(string inputKey)
    {
        var normalized = inputKey.Trim().ToUpper();
        var expected = ComputeKey(GetMachineId());

        if (normalized != expected) return false;

        WriteReg(VAL_LICENSE, expected);
        return true;
    }

    // ── Private helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    /// <summary>
    /// يحسب كود التفعيل المتوقع لهذا الجهاز.
    /// نفس الخوارزمية المستخدمة في KeyGeneratorTool.csx.
    /// </summary>
    private static string ComputeKey(string machineId)
    {
        var key = Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(SECRET);
        var data = Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(machineId.ToUpper());

        using var hmac = new HMACSHA256(key);
        var hash = hmac.ComputeHash(data);
        var hex = BitConverter.ToString(hash, 0, 8).Replace("-", "").ToUpper();

        return $"{hex[..4]}-{hex[4..8]}-{hex[8..12]}-{hex[12..16]}";
    }

    /// <summary>يجلب تاريخ أول تشغيل، أو يحفظ اليوم إذا كانت أول مرة.</summary>
    private static DateTime GetOrSetInstallDate()
    {
        var val = ReadReg(VAL_INSTALL);

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(val) &&
            DateTime.TryParseExact(val, "yyyyMMdd", null,
                System.Globalization.DateTimeStyles.None, out var date))
            return date;

        // أول تشغيل — سجّل التاريخ
        WriteReg(VAL_INSTALL, DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMdd"));
        return DateTime.Now;
    }

    private static string GetCpuId()
    {
        try
        {
            using var s = new ManagementObjectSearcher(
                "SELECT ProcessorId FROM Win32_Processor");
            foreach (ManagementObject o in s.Get())
                return o["ProcessorId"]?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? "CPU_UNKNOWN";
        }
        catch { /* WMI غير متاح */ }
        return "CPU_UNKNOWN";
    }

    private static string GetVolumeSerial()
    {
        try
        {
            var drive = Path.GetPathRoot(
                Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.System))
                ?.TrimEnd('\\');

            using var s = new ManagementObjectSearcher(
                $"SELECT VolumeSerialNumber FROM Win32_LogicalDisk WHERE DeviceID='{drive}'");
            foreach (ManagementObject o in s.Get())
                return o["VolumeSerialNumber"]?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? "VOL_UNKNOWN";
        }
        catch { /* WMI غير متاح */ }
        return "VOL_UNKNOWN";
    }

    private static string? ReadReg(string name)
    {
        try
        {
            using var k = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey(REG_PATH);
            return k?.GetValue(name)?.ToString();
        }
        catch { return null; }
    }

    private static void WriteReg(string name, string value)
    {
        try
        {
            using var k = Registry.CurrentUser.CreateSubKey(REG_PATH, writable: true);
            k?.SetValue(name, value);
        }
        catch { /* silent fail — مش هيأثر على التشغيل */ }
    }
}

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Models;


public class Invoice
{
    public int Id { get; set; }
    public string InvoiceNumber { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public DateTime Date { get; set; } = DateTime.Now;
    public PaymentType PaymentType { get; set; }

    public int CustomerId { get; set; }
    public Customer Customer { get; set; } = null!;

    public int? SafeId { get; set; }
    public Safe? Safe { get; set; }

    public decimal SubTotal { get; set; }
    public decimal DiscountAmount { get; set; }
    public decimal TotalAmount { get; set; }
    public decimal PaidAmount { get; set; }

    // ← جديد: المتبقي = الإجمالي - المدفوع
    public decimal RemainingAmount => TotalAmount - PaidAmount;

    public bool IsCancelled { get; set; }
    public string? CancelReason { get; set; }
    public string? Notes { get; set; }
    public DateTime CreatedAt { get; set; } = DateTime.Now;

    public ICollection<InvoiceLine> Lines { get; set; } = [];
    public ICollection<ReceiptVoucher> ReceiptVouchers { get; set; } = [];
    public ICollection<JournalEntry> JournalEntries { get; set; } = [];
}
