<file path="Printing/InvoicePrinter.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Models;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using QuestPDF.Fluent;
using QuestPDF.Helpers;
using QuestPDF.Infrastructure;
using System.IO;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Printing;

public static class InvoicePrinter
{
    private const string ColPrimary = "#C0392B";
    private const string ColDark = "#1D1D1F";
    private const string ColSecondary = "#6E6E73";
    private const string ColSurface = "#F5F5F7";
    private const string ColBorder = "#D2D2D7";
    private const string ColWhite = "#FFFFFF";
    private const string ColGreen = "#1E8449";
    private const string ColOrange = "#D35400";   // لون الخصم
    private const string ColBrand = "#8B7355";   // لون الـ branding

    public static async Task PrintAsync(int invoiceId)
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var invoice = await db.Invoices
            .Include(i => i.Customer)
            .Include(i => i.Lines)
            .FirstAsync(i => i.Id == invoiceId);

        // ── تحميل بيانات المؤسسة ──────────────────────────
        var org = await OrgInfoCache.GetAsync();

        byte[]? logoBytes = null;
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.LogoPath) && File.Exists(org.LogoPath))
            logoBytes = await File.ReadAllBytesAsync(org.LogoPath);

        var path = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"Invoice_{invoice.InvoiceNumber}.pdf");

        var doc = Document.Create(container =>
        {
            container.Page(page =>
            {
                page.Size(PageSizes.A4);
                page.Margin(2.2f, Unit.Centimetre);
                page.DefaultTextStyle(x => x
                    .FontFamily("Arial")
                    .FontSize(11)
                    .FontColor(ColDark)
                    .DirectionFromRightToLeft());

                page.Content().Column(col =>
                {
                    // ─── Header ───────────────────────────────────────────
                    col.Item().Row(row =>
                    {
                        // LEFT visual: info box
                        row.ConstantItem(200)
                            .Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                            .Background(ColSurface).Padding(12)
                            .Column(inner =>
                            {
                                inner.Item()
                                    .Text("فاتورة مبيعات")
                                    .FontSize(12).Bold().FontColor(ColPrimary).AlignCenter();

                                inner.Item().PaddingTop(8).Row(r =>
                                {
                                    r.RelativeItem().AlignRight()
                                        .Text(invoice.InvoiceNumber).Bold();
                                    r.ConstantItem(8);
                                    r.ConstantItem(80).AlignLeft()
                                        .Text("رقم الفاتورة").FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                                });
                                inner.Item().PaddingTop(4).Row(r =>
                                {
                                    r.RelativeItem().AlignRight()
                                        .Text(invoice.Date.ToString("d/M/yyyy"));
                                    r.ConstantItem(8);
                                    r.ConstantItem(80).AlignLeft()
                                        .Text("التاريخ").FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                                });
                            });

                        row.ConstantItem(16);

                        // RIGHT visual: bank name + optional logo
                        row.RelativeItem().Column(c =>
                        {
                            c.Item().Row(r =>
                            {
                                if (logoBytes is not null)
                                {
                                    r.ConstantItem(52).Height(52)
                                        .Image(logoBytes).FitArea();
                                    r.ConstantItem(10);
                                }
                                r.RelativeItem().Column(inner =>
                                {
                                    inner.Item()
                                        .Text(org.BankName)
                                        .FontSize(logoBytes is null ? 26 : 20)
                                        .Bold().FontColor(ColPrimary).AlignRight();
                                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.SubTitle))
                                        inner.Item().PaddingTop(3)
                                            .Text(org.SubTitle)
                                            .FontSize(10).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignRight();
                                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.Phone))
                                        inner.Item().PaddingTop(2)
                                            .Text($"📞  {org.Phone}")
                                            .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignRight();
                                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.Address))
                                        inner.Item()
                                            .Text(org.Address)
                                            .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignRight();
                                });
                            });
                        });
                    });

                    col.Item().PaddingVertical(14)
                        .LineHorizontal(0.5f).LineColor(ColBorder);

                    // ─── Customer Info ────────────────────────────────────
                    col.Item().Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder).Padding(14).Row(row =>
                    {
                        row.RelativeItem().Column(c =>
                        {
                            c.Item().Text("رقم الهاتف")
                                .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignLeft();
                            c.Item().PaddingTop(3)
                                .Text(invoice.Customer.Phone ?? "—")
                                .FontSize(13).Bold().AlignLeft();
                        });

                        row.ConstantItem(16);
                        row.ConstantItem(1).Background(ColBorder);
                        row.ConstantItem(16);

                        row.RelativeItem().Column(c =>
                        {
                            c.Item().Text("بيانات العميل")
                                .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignRight();
                            c.Item().PaddingTop(3)
                                .Text(invoice.Customer.Name)
                                .FontSize(13).Bold().AlignRight();
                        });
                    });

                    // ─── Payment & Notes ─────────────────────────────────
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(invoice.Notes) || invoice.PaymentType != PaymentType.Cash)
                    {
                        col.Item().PaddingTop(10).Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                            .Background(ColSurface).Padding(12).Row(row =>
                            {
                                row.RelativeItem().Column(c =>
                                {
                                    c.Item().Text("ملاحظات")
                                        .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignLeft();
                                    c.Item().PaddingTop(3)
                                        .Text(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(invoice.Notes) ? "—" : invoice.Notes)
                                        .FontSize(11).AlignLeft();
                                });

                                row.ConstantItem(1).Background(ColBorder);
                                row.ConstantItem(16);

                                row.ConstantItem(160).Column(c =>
                                {
                                    c.Item().Text("طريقة الدفع")
                                        .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignRight();
                                    c.Item().PaddingTop(3)
                                        .Text(invoice.PaymentType switch
                                        {
                                            PaymentType.Cash => "نقدي",
                                            PaymentType.Credit => "آجل",
                                            PaymentType.Partial => $"جزئي — مدفوع: {invoice.PaidAmount:N2} ج.م",
                                            _ => invoice.PaymentType.ToString()
                                        })
                                        .FontSize(11).Bold().AlignRight();
                                });
                            });
                    }

                    // ─── Lines Table ──────────────────────────────────────
                    col.Item().PaddingTop(18).Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder).Table(table =>
                    {
                        table.ColumnsDefinition(cols =>
                        {
                            cols.ConstantColumn(100);
                            cols.RelativeColumn(2);
                            cols.RelativeColumn(2);
                            cols.RelativeColumn(4);
                            cols.ConstantColumn(36);
                        });

                        static IContainer TH(IContainer c) =>
                            c.Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                             .Background(ColPrimary)
                             .Padding(10).AlignCenter();

                        table.Cell().Element(TH).Text("الإجمالي")
                            .FontColor(ColWhite).Bold();
                        table.Cell().Element(TH).Text("سعر الوحدة")
                            .FontColor(ColWhite).Bold();
                        table.Cell().Element(TH).Text("الكمية")
                            .FontColor(ColWhite).Bold();
                        table.Cell().Element(TH).Text("الصنف / المنتج")
                            .FontColor(ColWhite).Bold();
                        table.Cell().Element(TH).Text("#")
                            .FontColor(ColWhite).Bold();

                        int i = 1;
                        foreach (var line in invoice.Lines)
                        {
                            var bg = i % 2 == 0 ? ColSurface : ColWhite;

                            table.Cell().Element(c => TD(c, bg)).AlignCenter()
                                .Text($"{line.Total:N2} ج.م").Bold().FontColor(ColGreen);
                            table.Cell().Element(c => TD(c, bg)).AlignCenter()
                                .Text($"{line.UnitPrice:N2} ج.م");
                            table.Cell().Element(c => TD(c, bg)).AlignCenter()
                                .Text(line.Quantity.ToString());
                            table.Cell().Element(c => TD(c, bg)).AlignRight()
                                .Text(line.ProductName);
                            table.Cell().Element(c => TD(c, bg)).AlignCenter()
                                .Text(i.ToString()).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                            i++;
                        }

                        static IContainer TD(IContainer c, string bg) =>
                            c.Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder).Background(bg).Padding(10);
                    });

                    // ─── Totals Box ───────────────────────────────────────
                    // يعرض: إجمالي الأصناف / الخصم (إن وجد) / صافي الفاتورة
                    col.Item().PaddingTop(2).Row(row =>
                    {
                        row.RelativeItem(); // spacer يدفع الجدول لليمين

                        row.ConstantItem(300)
                            .Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                            .Background(ColSurface)
                            .Column(totals =>
                            {
                                // ── صف: إجمالي الأصناف ───────────────────
                                totals.Item()
                                    .BorderBottom(invoice.DiscountAmount > 0 ? 0.5f : 0f)
                                    .BorderColor(ColBorder)
                                    .Padding(12)
                                    .Row(r =>
                                    {
                                        r.RelativeItem().AlignRight()
                                            .Text($"{invoice.SubTotal:N2} ج.م")
                                            .FontSize(12).Bold().FontColor(ColDark);
                                        r.ConstantItem(8);
                                        r.ConstantItem(110).AlignLeft()
                                            .Text("إجمالي الأصناف")
                                            .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignCenter();
                                    });

                                // ── صف: الخصم — يظهر فقط إذا كان > 0 ────
                                if (invoice.DiscountAmount > 0)
                                {
                                    totals.Item()
                                        .BorderBottom(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                                        .Padding(12)
                                        .Row(r =>
                                        {
                                            r.RelativeItem().AlignRight()
                                                .Text(t =>
                                                {
                                                    t.Span("− ").FontColor(ColOrange).Bold();
                                                    t.Span($"{invoice.DiscountAmount:N2} ج.م")
                                                        .FontSize(12).Bold().FontColor(ColOrange);
                                                });
                                            r.ConstantItem(8);
                                            r.ConstantItem(110).AlignLeft()
                                                .Text("الخصم")
                                                .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignCenter();
                                        });
                                }

                                // ── صف: صافي الفاتورة ────────────────────
                                totals.Item()
                                    .Background(ColPrimary)
                                    .Padding(14)
                                    .Row(r =>
                                    {
                                        r.RelativeItem().AlignRight()
                                            .Text($"{invoice.TotalAmount:N2} ج.م")
                                            .FontSize(15).Bold().FontColor(ColWhite);
                                        r.ConstantItem(8);
                                        r.ConstantItem(110).AlignLeft()
                                            .Text("صافي الفاتورة")
                                            .FontSize(10).FontColor(ColWhite).AlignCenter();
                                    });
                            });
                    });

                    // ─── Thank-you + Branding ─────────────────────────────
                    col.Item().PaddingTop(20).AlignCenter()
                        .Text("شكراً لتعاملكم معنا")
                        .FontSize(10).FontColor(ColSecondary);

                    col.Item().PaddingTop(8).LineHorizontal(0.5f).LineColor("#EEEEEE");

                    col.Item().PaddingTop(6).AlignCenter()
                        .Text(t =>
                        {
                            t.Span("برنامج حسابات شريان  |  ")
                                .FontSize(8).FontColor(ColBrand);
                            t.Span("م. عبدالرحمن علوان")
                                .FontSize(8).FontColor(ColBrand).Bold();
                            t.Span("  ✦  01127136834")
                                .FontSize(8).FontColor(ColBrand);
                        });
                });
            });
        });

        doc.GeneratePdf(path);
        System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(
            new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo(path) { UseShellExecute = true });
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Printing/OrgInfoCache.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Models;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Printing;

/// <summary>
/// يُحمِّل إعدادات المؤسسة مرة واحدة ويخزّنها — استدعِ Invalidate()
/// بعد أي حفظ في OrganizationSettings لإعادة التحميل عند الطباعة التالية.
/// </summary>
public static class OrgInfoCache
{
    private static OrganizationSettings? _cached;

    public static async Task<OrganizationSettings> GetAsync()
    {
        if (_cached is not null) return _cached;
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        _cached = await db.OrgSettings.FirstOrDefaultAsync()
                  ?? new OrganizationSettings();
        return _cached;
    }

    public static void Invalidate() => _cached = null;
}
</file>

<file path="Printing/PaymentPrinter.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Helpers;
using BloodBankAccounting.Models;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using QuestPDF.Fluent;
using QuestPDF.Helpers;
using QuestPDF.Infrastructure;
using System.IO;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Printing;

public static class PaymentPrinter
{
    private const string ColPrimary = "#C0392B";
    private const string ColDark = "#1D1D1F";
    private const string ColSecondary = "#6E6E73";
    private const string ColSurface = "#F5F5F7";
    private const string ColBorder = "#D2D2D7";
    private const string ColWhite = "#FFFFFF";
    private const string ColBrand = "#8B7355";

    public static async Task PrintAsync(int voucherId)
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var v = await db.ReceiptVouchers
            .Include(r => r.Safe)
            .FirstAsync(r => r.Id == voucherId);

        // ── تحميل بيانات المؤسسة ──────────────────────────
        var org = await OrgInfoCache.GetAsync();

        byte[]? logoBytes = null;
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.LogoPath) && File.Exists(org.LogoPath))
            logoBytes = await File.ReadAllBytesAsync(org.LogoPath);

        var path = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"Payment_{v.VoucherNumber}.pdf");
        var inWords = ArabicNumberHelper.ToWords(v.Amount);

        var doc = Document.Create(container =>
        {
            container.Page(page =>
            {
                page.Size(PageSizes.A4);
                page.Margin(1.4f, Unit.Centimetre);
                page.DefaultTextStyle(x => x
                    .FontFamily("Arial")
                    .FontSize(11)
                    .FontColor(ColDark)
                    .DirectionFromRightToLeft());

                page.Content().Column(col =>
                {
                    string[] labels = { "نسخة العميل", "نسخة المحاسبة", "نسخة الأرشيف" };
                    for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
                    {
                        col.Item().Element(e => BuildCopy(e, v, org, logoBytes, inWords, labels[i]));
                        if (i < 2)
                            col.Item().PaddingVertical(6).AlignCenter()
                                .Text("- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -")
                                .FontSize(8).FontColor(ColBorder);
                    }
                });
            });
        });

        doc.GeneratePdf(path);
        System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(
            new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo(path) { UseShellExecute = true });
    }

    private static void BuildCopy(
        IContainer container,
        ReceiptVoucher v,
        Models.OrganizationSettings org,
        byte[]? logoBytes,
        string words,
        string label)
    {
        container.Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder).Padding(14).Column(col =>
        {
            // ── Header ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
            col.Item().Row(row =>
            {
                // LEFT: date + voucher number
                row.RelativeItem().Column(c =>
                {
                    c.Item().Text($"التاريخ: {DateTime.Now:d/M/yyyy}")
                        .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignLeft();
                    c.Item().PaddingTop(3)
                        .Text(v.VoucherNumber)
                        .FontSize(9).Bold().FontColor(ColDark).AlignLeft();
                });

                row.ConstantItem(16);

                // CENTER: voucher title
                row.ConstantItem(160).Column(c =>
                {
                    c.Item()
                        .Background(ColPrimary).Padding(8)
                        .AlignCenter()
                        .Text("سند دفع نقدي")
                        .FontColor(ColWhite).Bold().FontSize(13);
                    c.Item().PaddingTop(3)
                        .AlignCenter()
                        .Text(label)
                        .FontSize(8).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                });

                row.ConstantItem(16);

                // RIGHT: bank name + logo
                row.RelativeItem().Column(c =>
                {
                    c.Item().Row(r =>
                    {
                        r.RelativeItem().Column(inner =>
                        {
                            inner.Item()
                                .Text(org.BankName)
                                .FontSize(18).Bold().FontColor(ColPrimary).AlignRight();
                            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.SubTitle))
                                inner.Item().PaddingTop(2)
                                    .Text(org.SubTitle)
                                    .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignRight();
                        });
                        if (logoBytes is not null)
                        {
                            r.ConstantItem(6);
                            r.ConstantItem(38).Height(38).Image(logoBytes).FitArea();
                        }
                    });
                });
            });

            col.Item().PaddingVertical(10).LineHorizontal(0.5f).LineColor(ColBorder);

            // ── Details Table ────────────────────────────────────────────
            col.Item().Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder).Table(table =>
            {
                table.ColumnsDefinition(cols =>
                {
                    cols.RelativeColumn(3);
                    cols.ConstantColumn(1);
                    cols.ConstantColumn(120);
                });

                void Row(string lbl, string val, bool highlight = false)
                {
                    var valueCell = table.Cell()
                        .Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                        .Padding(10).AlignRight();

                    if (highlight)
                        valueCell.Text(val).Bold().FontSize(14).FontColor(ColPrimary);
                    else
                        valueCell.Text(val);

                    table.Cell().Background(ColBorder);

                    table.Cell()
                        .Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                        .Background(ColSurface).Padding(10).AlignRight()
                        .Text(lbl).FontSize(9).SemiBold().FontColor(ColSecondary);
                }

                Row("رقم السند", v.VoucherNumber);
                Row("التاريخ", v.Date.ToString("d/M/yyyy"));
                Row("الخزينة", v.Safe?.Name ?? "—");
                Row("صُرف إلى", string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(v.PayeeName) ? "—" : v.PayeeName);
                Row("المبلغ", $"{v.Amount:N2} ج.م", highlight: true);
                Row("المبلغ كتابةً", words);
                Row("البيان", string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(v.Notes) ? "—" : v.Notes);
            });

            col.Item().PaddingVertical(10);

            // ── Signatures ───────────────────────────────────────────────
            col.Item().Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder).Table(table =>
            {
                table.ColumnsDefinition(cols =>
                {
                    cols.RelativeColumn();
                    cols.ConstantColumn(1);
                    cols.RelativeColumn();
                    cols.ConstantColumn(1);
                    cols.RelativeColumn();
                });

                void SigCell(string title)
                {
                    table.Cell()
                        .Background(ColSurface).Padding(12)
                        .AlignCenter().Column(c =>
                        {
                            c.Item().Text(title)
                                .FontSize(9).SemiBold()
                                .FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignCenter();
                            c.Item().PaddingTop(20)
                                .Text("_________________")
                                .FontColor(ColBorder).AlignCenter();
                        });
                }

                SigCell("المدير");
                table.Cell().Background(ColBorder);
                SigCell("المحاسب");
                table.Cell().Background(ColBorder);
                SigCell("أمين الخزينة");
            });

            // ── Branding ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
            col.Item().PaddingTop(6).AlignCenter()
                .Text(t =>
                {
                    t.Span("برنامج حسابات شريان  |  ")
                        .FontSize(7).FontColor(ColBrand);
                    t.Span("م. عبدالرحمن علوان")
                        .FontSize(7).FontColor(ColBrand).Bold();
                    t.Span("  ✦  01127136834")
                        .FontSize(7).FontColor(ColBrand);
                });
        });
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Printing/ReceiptPrinter.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using QuestPDF.Fluent;
using QuestPDF.Helpers;
using QuestPDF.Infrastructure;
using System.IO;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Printing;

public static class ReceiptPrinter
{
    private const string ColPrimary = "#C0392B";
    private const string ColDark = "#1D1D1F";
    private const string ColSecondary = "#6E6E73";
    private const string ColSurface = "#F5F5F7";
    private const string ColBorder = "#D2D2D7";
    private const string ColWhite = "#FFFFFF";
    private const string ColBrand = "#8B7355";

    public static async Task PrintAsync(int voucherId)
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var v = await db.ReceiptVouchers
            .Include(r => r.Customer)
            .Include(r => r.Safe)
            .FirstAsync(r => r.Id == voucherId);

        // ── تحميل بيانات المؤسسة ──────────────────────────
        var org = await OrgInfoCache.GetAsync();

        byte[]? logoBytes = null;
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.LogoPath) && File.Exists(org.LogoPath))
            logoBytes = await File.ReadAllBytesAsync(org.LogoPath);

        var path = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"Receipt_{v.VoucherNumber}.pdf");

        var doc = Document.Create(container =>
        {
            container.Page(page =>
            {
                page.Size(PageSizes.A5);
                page.Margin(1.8f, Unit.Centimetre);
                page.DefaultTextStyle(x => x
                    .FontFamily("Arial")
                    .FontSize(11)
                    .DirectionFromRightToLeft());

                page.Content().Column(col =>
                {
                    // ── Header: شعار + اسم البنك ──────────────────────
                    col.Item().Row(row =>
                    {
                        if (logoBytes is not null)
                        {
                            row.ConstantItem(44).Height(44).Image(logoBytes).FitArea();
                            row.ConstantItem(8);
                        }
                        row.RelativeItem().Column(inner =>
                        {
                            inner.Item().AlignCenter()
                                .Text(org.BankName)
                                .FontSize(22).Bold().FontColor(ColPrimary);
                            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.SubTitle))
                                inner.Item().AlignCenter()
                                    .Text(org.SubTitle)
                                    .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                        });
                    });

                    col.Item().PaddingVertical(10)
                        .Background(ColDark).Padding(8).AlignCenter()
                        .Text("سند قبض").FontColor(ColWhite).Bold();

                    col.Item().Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder).Table(table =>
                    {
                        table.ColumnsDefinition(cols =>
                        {
                            cols.RelativeColumn();
                            cols.ConstantColumn(110);
                        });
                        AddRow(table, "رقم السند", v.VoucherNumber);
                        AddRow(table, "التاريخ", v.Date.ToString("d/M/yyyy"));
                        AddRow(table, "الخزينة", v.Safe?.Name ?? "—");
                        AddRow(table, "العميل", v.Customer?.Name ?? "—");
                        AddRow(table, "المبلغ", $"{v.Amount:N2} ج.م", highlight: true);
                        AddRow(table, "البيان", string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(v.Notes) ? "—" : v.Notes);
                    });

                    // ── Branding footer ────────────────────────────────
                    col.Item().PaddingTop(16).LineHorizontal(0.5f).LineColor("#EEEEEE");
                    col.Item().PaddingTop(6).AlignCenter()
                        .Text(t =>
                        {
                            t.Span("برنامج حسابات شريان  |  ")
                                .FontSize(7.5f).FontColor(ColBrand);
                            t.Span("م. عبدالرحمن علوان")
                                .FontSize(7.5f).FontColor(ColBrand).Bold();
                            t.Span("  ✦  01127136834")
                                .FontSize(7.5f).FontColor(ColBrand);
                        });
                });
            });
        });

        doc.GeneratePdf(path);
        System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(
            new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo(path) { UseShellExecute = true });
    }

    private static void AddRow(TableDescriptor table, string label, string value, bool highlight = false)
    {
        table.Cell().Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder).Padding(10).AlignRight()
            .Text(value).FontColor(highlight ? ColPrimary : ColDark);
        table.Cell().Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder).Background(ColSurface)
            .Padding(10).AlignLeft().Text(label).SemiBold();
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Printing/SafeStatementPrinter.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels;
using QuestPDF.Fluent;
using QuestPDF.Helpers;
using QuestPDF.Infrastructure;
using System.IO;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Printing;

public static class SafeStatementPrinter
{
    private const string ColPrimary = "#C0392B";
    private const string ColDark = "#1D1D1F";
    private const string ColSecondary = "#6E6E73";
    private const string ColSurface = "#F5F5F7";
    private const string ColBorder = "#D2D2D7";
    private const string ColWhite = "#FFFFFF";
    private const string ColGreen = "#1E8449";
    private const string ColRed = "#7B241C";
    private const string ColBlue = "#1A5276";
    private const string ColBrand = "#8B7355";

    public static async Task PrintAsync(
        string safeName,
        DateTime from,
        DateTime to,
        decimal openingBalance,
        decimal closingBalance,
        decimal totalDebit,
        decimal totalCredit,
        List<SafeEntryRow> entries)
    {
        // ── تحميل بيانات المؤسسة ──────────────────────────
        var org = await OrgInfoCache.GetAsync();

        byte[]? logoBytes = null;
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.LogoPath) && File.Exists(org.LogoPath))
            logoBytes = await File.ReadAllBytesAsync(org.LogoPath);

        await Task.Run(() =>
        {
            var fileName = $"كشف_حساب_{safeName}_{from:yyyyMMdd}_{to:yyyyMMdd}.pdf";
            var path = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), fileName);

            var doc = Document.Create(container =>
            {
                container.Page(page =>
                {
                    page.Size(PageSizes.A4);
                    page.MarginTop(1.5f, Unit.Centimetre);
                    page.MarginBottom(1.5f, Unit.Centimetre);
                    page.MarginHorizontal(1.8f, Unit.Centimetre);

                    page.DefaultTextStyle(x => x
                        .FontFamily("Arial")
                        .FontSize(10)
                        .FontColor(ColDark)
                        .DirectionFromRightToLeft());

                    page.Content().Column(col =>
                    {
                        col.Item().Element(BuildHeader(safeName, from, to, org, logoBytes));
                        col.Item().PaddingVertical(12).LineHorizontal(0.5f).LineColor(ColBorder);
                        col.Item().Element(BuildSummaryRow(openingBalance, totalDebit, totalCredit, closingBalance));
                        col.Item().PaddingVertical(14);
                        col.Item().Element(BuildTable(entries));
                        col.Item().PaddingTop(16).Element(BuildFooter());

                        // ── Branding ──────────────────────────────────
                        col.Item().PaddingTop(8).LineHorizontal(0.5f).LineColor("#EEEEEE");
                        col.Item().PaddingTop(5).AlignCenter()
                            .Text(t =>
                            {
                                t.Span("برنامج حسابات شريان  |  ")
                                    .FontSize(7.5f).FontColor(ColBrand);
                                t.Span("م. عبدالرحمن علوان")
                                    .FontSize(7.5f).FontColor(ColBrand).Bold();
                                t.Span("  ✦  01127136834")
                                    .FontSize(7.5f).FontColor(ColBrand);
                            });
                    });

                    page.Footer().AlignCenter().Text(t =>
                    {
                        t.Span("صفحة ").FontSize(8).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                        t.CurrentPageNumber().FontSize(8).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                        t.Span(" من ").FontSize(8).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                        t.TotalPages().FontSize(8).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                        t.Span($"   |   طُبع في: {DateTime.Now:d/M/yyyy  HH:mm}")
                            .FontSize(8).FontColor(ColBorder);
                    });
                });
            });

            doc.GeneratePdf(path);
            System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(
                new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo(path) { UseShellExecute = true });
        });
    }

    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  رأس الصفحة
    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    private static Action<IContainer> BuildHeader(
        string safeName, DateTime from, DateTime to,
        Models.OrganizationSettings org, byte[]? logoBytes)
        => c => c.Row(row =>
        {
            row.ConstantItem(230)
                .Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                .Background(ColSurface).Padding(12)
                .Column(inner =>
                {
                    inner.Item().Row(r =>
                    {
                        r.RelativeItem().AlignRight().Text(safeName).Bold();
                        r.RelativeItem().AlignLeft().Text("الخزينة").FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                    });
                    inner.Item().PaddingTop(4).Row(r =>
                    {
                        r.RelativeItem().AlignRight().Text(from.ToString("d/M/yyyy"));
                        r.RelativeItem().AlignLeft().Text("من").FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                    });
                    inner.Item().PaddingTop(4).Row(r =>
                    {
                        r.RelativeItem().AlignRight().Text(to.ToString("d/M/yyyy"));
                        r.RelativeItem().AlignLeft().Text("إلى").FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                    });
                });

            row.RelativeItem().Column(inner =>
            {
                inner.Item().Row(r =>
                {
                    r.RelativeItem().Column(bc =>
                    {
                        bc.Item()
                            .Text(org.BankName)
                            .FontSize(22).Bold().FontColor(ColPrimary);
                        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.SubTitle))
                            bc.Item().PaddingTop(2)
                                .Text("كشف حساب خزينة — " + org.SubTitle)
                                .FontSize(10).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                        else
                            bc.Item().PaddingTop(2)
                                .Text("كشف حساب خزينة")
                                .FontSize(11).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.Phone))
                            bc.Item().Text($"📞  {org.Phone}")
                                .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary);
                    });
                    if (logoBytes is not null)
                    {
                        r.ConstantItem(8);
                        r.ConstantItem(52).Height(52).Image(logoBytes).FitArea();
                    }
                });
            });
        });

    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  ملخص الأرصدة
    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    private static Action<IContainer> BuildSummaryRow(
        decimal openingBalance, decimal totalDebit, decimal totalCredit, decimal closingBalance)
        => c => c.Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder).Table(table =>
        {
            table.ColumnsDefinition(cols =>
            {
                cols.RelativeColumn();
                cols.ConstantColumn(1);
                cols.RelativeColumn();
                cols.ConstantColumn(1);
                cols.RelativeColumn();
                cols.ConstantColumn(1);
                cols.RelativeColumn();
            });

            void Card(string label, decimal value, string color)
            {
                table.Cell()
                    .Background(ColSurface).Padding(12)
                    .AlignCenter().Column(cc =>
                    {
                        cc.Item().Text(label)
                            .FontSize(9).FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignCenter();
                        cc.Item().PaddingTop(5)
                            .Text($"{value:N2} ج.م")
                            .FontSize(13).Bold().FontColor(color).AlignCenter();
                    });
            }

            void Divider() => table.Cell().Background(ColBorder);

            Card("الرصيد الختامي", closingBalance, closingBalance >= 0 ? ColGreen : ColPrimary);
            Divider();
            Card("إجمالي الصادر", totalCredit, ColRed);
            Divider();
            Card("إجمالي الوارد", totalDebit, ColGreen);
            Divider();
            Card("الرصيد الافتتاحي", openingBalance, ColBlue);
        });

    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  جدول الحركات
    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    private static Action<IContainer> BuildTable(List<SafeEntryRow> entries)
        => c => c.Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder).Table(table =>
        {
            table.ColumnsDefinition(cols =>
            {
                cols.ConstantColumn(105);
                cols.ConstantColumn(95);
                cols.ConstantColumn(95);
                cols.ConstantColumn(62);
                cols.RelativeColumn(3);
                cols.ConstantColumn(78);
            });

            void TH(string text)
            {
                table.Cell()
                    .Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                    .Background(ColDark).Padding(9)
                    .AlignCenter()
                    .Text(t => t.Span(text).FontColor(ColWhite).Bold().FontSize(10));
            }

            TH("الرصيد");
            TH("صادر  ▼");
            TH("وارد  ▲");
            TH("النوع");
            TH("البيان / الوصف");
            TH("التاريخ");

            int i = 0;
            foreach (var e in entries)
            {
                var bg = i % 2 == 0 ? ColWhite : ColSurface;

                void TD(string txt, string? color = null, bool bold = false, bool center = false)
                {
                    var cell = table.Cell()
                        .Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                        .Background(bg).Padding(7);

                    IContainer aligned = center ? cell.AlignCenter() : (IContainer)cell;
                    aligned.Text(t =>
                    {
                        var span = t.Span(txt).FontSize(9.5f);
                        if (color != null) span = span.FontColor(color);
                        if (bold) span = span.Bold();
                    });
                }

                var balColor = e.Balance >= 0 ? ColGreen : ColPrimary;
                TD($"{e.Balance:N2}", color: balColor, bold: true);

                if (e.Credit > 0)
                    TD($"{e.Credit:N2}", color: ColRed, bold: true);
                else
                    TD("—", color: ColBorder, center: true);

                if (e.Debit > 0)
                    TD($"{e.Debit:N2}", color: ColGreen, bold: true);
                else
                    TD("—", color: ColBorder, center: true);

                TD(e.RefTypeDisplay, center: true, color: ColSecondary);
                TD(e.Description);
                TD(e.Date.ToString("d/M/yyyy"), center: true, color: ColSecondary);

                i++;
            }

            // صف الإجمالي
            void TF(string txt, string bg2 = "#1D1D1F", string color = "#FFFFFF")
            {
                table.Cell()
                    .Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                    .Background(bg2).Padding(9)
                    .Text(t => t.Span(txt).FontColor(color).FontSize(10).Bold());
            }

            TF(entries.Count > 0 ? $"{entries.Last().Balance:N2} ج.م" : "0.00 ج.م",
               entries.Count > 0 && entries.Last().Balance >= 0 ? ColBlue : ColRed,
               ColWhite);
            TF($"{entries.Sum(e => e.Credit):N2} ج.م", ColRed, ColWhite);
            TF($"{entries.Sum(e => e.Debit):N2} ج.م", ColGreen, ColWhite);
            TF("", ColDark, ColWhite);
            TF("الإجمالي", ColDark, ColWhite);
            TF("", ColDark, ColWhite);
        });

    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  ذيل الصفحة — التوقيعات
    // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    private static Action<IContainer> BuildFooter()
        => c => c.Column(col =>
        {
            col.Item().LineHorizontal(0.5f).LineColor(ColBorder);
            col.Item().PaddingTop(14)
                .Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                .Table(table =>
                {
                    table.ColumnsDefinition(cols =>
                    {
                        cols.RelativeColumn();
                        cols.ConstantColumn(1);
                        cols.RelativeColumn();
                        cols.ConstantColumn(1);
                        cols.RelativeColumn();
                    });

                    void SigCell(string title)
                    {
                        table.Cell()
                            .Border(0.5f).BorderColor(ColBorder)
                            .Background(ColSurface).Padding(14)
                            .AlignCenter().Column(c2 =>
                            {
                                c2.Item().Text(title)
                                    .FontSize(10).SemiBold()
                                    .FontColor(ColSecondary).AlignCenter();
                                c2.Item().PaddingTop(22)
                                    .Text("_________________")
                                    .FontColor(ColBorder).AlignCenter();
                            });
                    }

                    SigCell("المدير");
                    table.Cell().Background(ColBorder);
                    SigCell("المحاسب");
                    table.Cell().Background(ColBorder);
                    SigCell("أمين الخزينة");
                });
        });
}

// ── Extension helpers ──────────────────────────────────────
internal static class QuestExtensions
{
    internal static IContainer When(
        this IContainer c,
        bool condition,
        Func<IContainer, IContainer> apply)
        => condition ? apply(c) : c;

    internal static TextSpanDescriptor When(
        this TextSpanDescriptor t,
        bool condition,
        Func<TextSpanDescriptor, TextSpanDescriptor> apply)
        => condition ? apply(t) : t;
}

<file path="ViewModels/AllViewModels.cs">

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  CUSTOMERS
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
public partial class CustomersViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    public ObservableCollection<Customer> Customers { get; } = [];

    [ObservableProperty] private Customer? _selected;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _filterText = string.Empty;

    // Form fields
    [ObservableProperty] private string _name = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private CustomerType _type = CustomerType.Hospital;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _phone = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _creditLimit;
    [ObservableProperty] private bool _isEditing;

    public IEnumerable<CustomerType> CustomerTypes { get; } = Enum.GetValues<CustomerType>();

    public CustomersViewModel() { _ = LoadAsync(); }

    private async Task LoadAsync()
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var list = await db.Customers.OrderBy(c => c.Name).ToListAsync();
        Customers.Clear();
        list.ForEach(Customers.Add);
    }

    partial void OnSelectedChanged(Customer? value)
    {
        if (value is null) return;
        Name = value.Name;
        Type = value.Type;
        Phone = value.Phone;
        CreditLimit = value.CreditLimit;
        IsEditing = true;
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void NewCustomer()
    {
        Selected = null;
        Name = string.Empty;
        Phone = string.Empty;
        CreditLimit = 0;
        Type = CustomerType.Hospital;
        IsEditing = true;
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task SaveAsync()
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Name)) { ShowError("اسم العميل مطلوب"); return; }
        using var db = new AppDbContext();

        if (Selected is null)
        {
            db.Customers.Add(new Customer { Name = Name, Type = Type, Phone = Phone, CreditLimit = CreditLimit });
        }
        else
        {
            var c = await db.Customers.FindAsync(Selected.Id);
            c!.Name = Name; c.Type = Type; c.Phone = Phone; c.CreditLimit = CreditLimit;
        }

        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
        IsEditing = false;
        await LoadAsync();
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task SearchAsync()
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var q = FilterText.Trim();
        var list = await db.Customers
            .Where(c => string.IsNullOrEmpty(q) || c.Name.Contains(q) || c.Phone.Contains(q))
            .OrderBy(c => c.Name).ToListAsync();
        Customers.Clear();
        list.ForEach(Customers.Add);
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  BLOOD INVENTORY
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
public partial class BloodInventoryViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    public ObservableCollection<BloodBag> Bags { get; } = [];

    // Form
    [ObservableProperty] private string _bagNumber = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private BloodGroup _bloodGroup = BloodGroup.APositive;
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _costPrice;
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _salePrice;
    [ObservableProperty] private DateTime _expiryDate = DateTime.Today.AddDays(35);
    [ObservableProperty] private BloodBagStatus _filterStatus = BloodBagStatus.Available;

    public IEnumerable<BloodGroup> BloodGroups { get; } = Enum.GetValues<BloodGroup>();
    public IEnumerable<BloodBagStatus> StatusOptions { get; } = Enum.GetValues<BloodBagStatus>();

    public BloodInventoryViewModel() { _ = LoadAsync(); }

    partial void OnFilterStatusChanged(BloodBagStatus value) { _ = LoadAsync(); }

    private async Task LoadAsync()
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var list = await db.BloodBags
                               .Where(b => b.Status == FilterStatus)
                               .OrderBy(b => b.ExpiryDate)
                               .ToListAsync();
        Bags.Clear();
        list.ForEach(Bags.Add);
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task AddBagAsync()
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(BagNumber)) { ShowError("رقم الكيس مطلوب"); return; }
        if (SalePrice <= 0) { ShowError("سعر البيع مطلوب"); return; }

        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        if (await db.BloodBags.AnyAsync(b => b.BagNumber == BagNumber))
        { ShowError("رقم الكيس مستخدم بالفعل"); return; }

        db.BloodBags.Add(new BloodBag
        {
            BagNumber = BagNumber,
            BloodGroup = BloodGroup,
            CostPrice = CostPrice,
            SalePrice = SalePrice,
            ExpiryDate = ExpiryDate
        });
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();

        BagNumber = string.Empty;
        await LoadAsync();
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  INVOICE LIST
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
public partial class InvoiceListViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    public ObservableCollection<Invoice> Invoices { get; } = [];

    // ─── كل الفواتير في الذاكرة للفلترة الفورية ──────────────────────
    private List<Invoice> _allInvoices = [];

    [ObservableProperty] private DateTime _from = DateTime.Today.AddDays(-2);
    [ObservableProperty] private DateTime _to = DateTime.Today;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _searchText = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private int _resultCount;

    partial void OnSearchTextChanged(string _) => FilterInvoices();

    private void FilterInvoices()
    {
        var q = SearchText.Trim();
        var filtered = string.IsNullOrEmpty(q)
            ? _allInvoices
            : _allInvoices.Where(i =>
                i.InvoiceNumber.Contains(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                (i.Customer?.Name?.Contains(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == true) ||
                i.TotalAmount.ToString("N2").Contains(q) ||
                i.PaidAmount.ToString("N2").Contains(q) ||
                i.Date.ToString("d/M/yyyy").Contains(q) ||
                (i.PaymentType == PaymentType.Cash && "نقدي".Contains(q)) ||
                (i.PaymentType == PaymentType.Credit && "آجل".Contains(q)) ||
                (i.PaymentType == PaymentType.Partial && "جزئي".Contains(q)) ||
                (i.IsCancelled && "ملغي".Contains(q)) ||
                (!i.IsCancelled && "مكتمل".Contains(q))
            ).ToList();

        Invoices.Clear();
        filtered.ForEach(Invoices.Add);
        ResultCount = filtered.Count;
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void ClearSearch() => SearchText = string.Empty;

    public InvoiceListViewModel() { _ = LoadAsync(); }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task LoadAsync()
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        _allInvoices = await db.Invoices
                               .Include(i => i.Customer)
                               .Where(i => i.Date.Date >= From && i.Date.Date <= To)
                               .OrderByDescending(i => i.Date)
                               .ToListAsync();
        // نستدعي FilterInvoices مباشرة بدل الاعتماد على SearchText change
        // لأن لو SearchText فاضية أصلاً مش هيطلق الـ event
        FilterInvoices();
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task PrintInvoiceAsync(Invoice invoice)
    {
        await InvoicePrinter.PrintAsync(invoice.Id);
    }

    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  استبدل الـ method الموجودة في InvoiceListViewModel
    //  السطور 277–312 في AllViewModels.cs
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task CancelInvoiceAsync(Invoice invoice)
    {
        // ── 1. Validation أساسي ──────────────────────────────────
        if (invoice.IsCancelled)
        {
            ShowError($"الفاتورة {invoice.InvoiceNumber} ملغاة بالفعل.");
            return;
        }

        // ── 2. طلب سبب الإلغاء ──────────────────────────────────
        var reason = Views.InputDialog.Show(
            $"سبب إلغاء الفاتورة {invoice.InvoiceNumber}",
            "إلغاء فاتورة",
            System.Windows.Application.Current.MainWindow);

        if (reason is null) return;          // المستخدم ضغط إلغاء
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(reason))
        {
            ShowError("يجب إدخال سبب الإلغاء.");
            return;
        }

        // ── 3. تأكيد نهائي ───────────────────────────────────────
        if (!Confirm(
            $"تأكيد إلغاء الفاتورة {invoice.InvoiceNumber}\n" +
            $"السبب: {reason}\n\n" +
            "سيتم عكس جميع الأثار المالية — هل أنت متأكد؟"))
            return;

        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            using var db = new AppDbContext();
            await using var tx = await db.Database.BeginTransactionAsync();

            // تحميل الفاتورة مع كل البيانات المرتبطة
            var inv = await db.Invoices
                .Include(i => i.Lines)
                .Include(i => i.JournalEntries)
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(i => i.Id == invoice.Id);

            if (inv is null)
            {
                ShowError("لم يتم العثور على الفاتورة في قاعدة البيانات.");
                return;
            }

            // تحقق مجدداً (race condition protection)
            if (inv.IsCancelled)
            {
                ShowError($"الفاتورة {inv.InvoiceNumber} ملغاة بالفعل.");
                return;
            }

            // ── 4. عكس حالة أكياس الدم ───────────────────────────
            foreach (var line in inv.Lines)
            {
                if (!line.BloodBagId.HasValue) continue;   // صنف يدوي — لا شيء
                var bag = await db.BloodBags.FindAsync(line.BloodBagId.Value);
                if (bag is not null) bag.Status = BloodBagStatus.Available;
            }

            // ── 5. عكس الأثر المالي حسب نوع الدفع ───────────────
            switch (inv.PaymentType)
            {
                // نقدي: المبلغ كله دخل الخزينة → نخصمه منها
                case PaymentType.Cash:
                    if (inv.SafeId.HasValue)
                    {
                        var safeCash = await db.Safes.FindAsync(inv.SafeId.Value);
                        if (safeCash is not null)
                            safeCash.Balance -= inv.TotalAmount;
                    }
                    break;

                // آجل: المبلغ كله أُضيف لدين العميل → نخصمه منه
                case PaymentType.Credit:
                    var cCredit = await db.Customers.FindAsync(inv.CustomerId);
                    if (cCredit is not null)
                        cCredit.Balance -= inv.TotalAmount;
                    break;

                // جزئي: جزء دخل الخزينة + جزء دين على العميل
                case PaymentType.Partial:
                    var cPartial = await db.Customers.FindAsync(inv.CustomerId);
                    if (cPartial is not null)
                        cPartial.Balance -= inv.RemainingAmount;   // الدين المتبقي

                    if (inv.SafeId.HasValue)
                    {
                        var safePartial = await db.Safes.FindAsync(inv.SafeId.Value);
                        if (safePartial is not null)
                            safePartial.Balance -= inv.PaidAmount;   // المقبوض فعلاً
                    }
                    break;
            }

            // ── 6. حذف القيود المحاسبية المرتبطة ────────────────
            if (inv.JournalEntries.Any())
                db.JournalEntries.RemoveRange(inv.JournalEntries);

            // ── 7. تسجيل الإلغاء ─────────────────────────────────
            inv.IsCancelled = true;
            inv.CancelReason = reason.Trim();

            await db.SaveChangesAsync();
            await tx.CommitAsync();

            ShowSuccess($"تم إلغاء الفاتورة {inv.InvoiceNumber} وعكس جميع آثارها المالية ✓");
            await LoadAsync();
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            var msg = ex.InnerException?.Message ?? ex.Message;
            ShowError($"حدث خطأ أثناء الإلغاء:\n{msg}");
        }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  DEBT REPORT
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
public partial class DebtReportViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    public ObservableCollection<Customer> Debtors { get; } = [];
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _totalDebt;

    public DebtReportViewModel() { _ = LoadAsync(); }

    private async Task LoadAsync()
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var list = await db.Customers
                               .Where(c => c.Balance > 0)
                               .OrderByDescending(c => c.Balance)
                               .ToListAsync();
        Debtors.Clear();
        list.ForEach(Debtors.Add);
        TotalDebt = list.Sum(c => c.Balance);
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  ORGANIZATION SETTINGS  (الإعدادات — جديد)
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
public partial class OrgSettingsViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    // ─── بيانات البنك ─────────────────────────────────────────
    [ObservableProperty] private string _bankName = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _subTitle = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _address = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _phone = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private string? _logoPath;
    [ObservableProperty] private bool _hasLogo;

    // ─── الرمز السري ──────────────────────────────────────────
    [ObservableProperty] private bool _hasPin;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _pinStatusMessage = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private bool _hasPinError;

    private string _currentPinHash = string.Empty;

    public OrgSettingsViewModel() { _ = LoadAsync(); }

    private async Task LoadAsync()
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var org = await db.OrgSettings.FirstOrDefaultAsync()
                  ?? new OrganizationSettings();

        BankName = org.BankName;
        SubTitle = org.SubTitle;
        Address = org.Address;
        Phone = org.Phone;
        LogoPath = org.LogoPath;
        HasLogo = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(org.LogoPath) && System.IO.File.Exists(org.LogoPath);

        _currentPinHash = org.OwnerPinHash;
        HasPin = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_currentPinHash);
    }

    // ─── حفظ بيانات البنك ─────────────────────────────────────
    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task SaveOrgInfoAsync()
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(BankName))
        {
            ShowError("اسم بنك الدم مطلوب");
            return;
        }

        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            using var db = new AppDbContext();
            var org = await db.OrgSettings.FirstOrDefaultAsync();

            if (org is null)
            {
                org = new OrganizationSettings { Id = 1 };
                db.OrgSettings.Add(org);
            }

            org.BankName = BankName.Trim();
            org.SubTitle = SubTitle.Trim();
            org.Address = Address.Trim();
            org.Phone = Phone.Trim();
            org.LogoPath = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(LogoPath) ? null : LogoPath.Trim();

            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            OrgInfoCache.Invalidate();   // إعادة تحميل عند الطباعة التالية
            StatusMessage = "✓  تم حفظ بيانات البنك بنجاح";
        }
        catch (Exception ex) { ShowError(ex.Message); }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }

    // ─── شعار البنك ───────────────────────────────────────────
    [RelayCommand]
    private void BrowseLogo()
    {
        var dlg = new OpenFileDialog
        {
            Title = "اختر صورة شعار البنك",
            Filter = "صور (*.png;*.jpg;*.jpeg;*.bmp)|*.png;*.jpg;*.jpeg;*.bmp"
        };
        if (dlg.ShowDialog() != true) return;

        LogoPath = dlg.FileName;
        HasLogo = true;
        StatusMessage = string.Empty;
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void RemoveLogo()
    {
        LogoPath = null;
        HasLogo = false;
    }

    // ─── الرمز السري (يُستدعى من Code-behind لأن PasswordBox لا يدعم Binding) ──
    public void ChangePinFromView(string currentPin, string newPin)
    {
        PinStatusMessage = string.Empty;
        HasPinError = false;

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(newPin))
        {
            PinStatusMessage = "أدخل الرمز الجديد أولاً";
            HasPinError = true;
            return;
        }

        // إذا كان هناك رمز سابق — تحقق منه أولاً
        if (HasPin && !PinHelper.Verify(currentPin, _currentPinHash))
        {
            PinStatusMessage = "الرمز الحالي غير صحيح";
            HasPinError = true;
            return;
        }

        _ = SaveNewPinAsync(newPin);
    }

    public void RemovePinFromView(string currentPin)
    {
        PinStatusMessage = string.Empty;
        HasPinError = false;

        if (!PinHelper.Verify(currentPin, _currentPinHash))
        {
            PinStatusMessage = "الرمز الحالي غير صحيح";
            HasPinError = true;
            return;
        }

        _ = SaveNewPinAsync(string.Empty);   // رمز فارغ = بدون حماية
    }

    private async Task SaveNewPinAsync(string newPin)
    {
        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            var newHash = PinHelper.Hash(newPin);

            using var db = new AppDbContext();
            var org = await db.OrgSettings.FirstOrDefaultAsync();
            if (org is null) { org = new OrganizationSettings { Id = 1 }; db.OrgSettings.Add(org); }

            org.OwnerPinHash = newHash;
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            _currentPinHash = newHash;
            HasPin = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(newHash);
            PinStatusMessage = HasPin ? "✓  تم حفظ الرمز السري بنجاح" : "✓  تم إزالة الرمز السري";
            HasPinError = false;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            PinStatusMessage = ex.Message;
            HasPinError = true;
        }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }
}   // ← end of OrgSettingsViewModel




// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  CHART DATA CLASSES  (لا تغيير عليهم)
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/// <summary>نقطة بيانات يومية لرسم مبيعات وإيرادات آخر 7 أيام</summary>
public class DailySalesPoint
{
    public string Label { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public string DateLabel { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public decimal Sales { get; set; }
    public decimal Revenue { get; set; }
    public bool IsToday { get; set; }

    public double SalesBarHeight { get; set; }
    public double SalesSpacerHeight { get; set; }
    public double RevenueBarHeight { get; set; }
    public double RevenueSpacerHeight { get; set; }
}

/// <summary>فصيلة دم واحدة لرسم التوزيع الأفقي</summary>
public class BloodGroupItem
{
    public string GroupName { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public int Count { get; set; }
    public double BarWidth { get; set; }
    public System.Windows.Media.SolidColorBrush Brush { get; set; }
        = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.DarkRed;
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  DASHBOARD VIEW MODEL  — استبدل الكلاس الموجود بهذا
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
public partial class DashboardViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    // ─── إحصائيات اليوم ──────────────────────────────────────
    /// <summary>إجمالي قيمة الفواتير المُصدرة اليوم</summary>
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _todaySales;

    /// <summary>الفلوس الداخلة فعلاً اليوم — نقدي + جزئي + سندات قبض</summary>
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _todayRevenue;

    [ObservableProperty] private int _todayInvoicesCount;

    /// <summary>فواتير نقدي + جزئي اليوم (تم تحصيلها)</summary>
    [ObservableProperty] private int _todayCashInvoices;

    /// <summary>فواتير آجل اليوم (لم تُحصَّل بعد)</summary>
    [ObservableProperty] private int _todayCreditInvoices;

    /// <summary>نسبة الإيرادات من المبيعات اليوم (0–100+)</summary>
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _collectionRateToday;

    // ─── إحصائيات الشهر ──────────────────────────────────────
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _monthSales;
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _monthRevenue;
    [ObservableProperty] private int _monthInvoicesCount;

    /// <summary>متوسط المبيعات اليومي هذا الشهر</summary>
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _averageDailySales;

    /// <summary>نسبة التحصيل الشهرية (0–100+)</summary>
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _collectionRateMonth;

    // ─── مؤشرات مستمرة ───────────────────────────────────────
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _totalDebt;
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _safeBalance;
    [ObservableProperty] private int _availableBags;
    [ObservableProperty] private int _expiringBags;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _topDebtorName = "—";
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _topDebtorBalance;

    // ─── بيانات الرسوم البيانية ───────────────────────────────
    public ObservableCollection<DailySalesPoint> WeeklySales { get; } = [];
    public ObservableCollection<BloodGroupItem> BloodGroupStats { get; } = [];

    // ─── القفل المالي ─────────────────────────────────────────
    [ObservableProperty] private bool _isFinancialUnlocked = true;
    private string _ownerPinHash = string.Empty;

    public string LockIcon => IsFinancialUnlocked ? "🔓" : "🔒";
    public string LockButtonText => IsFinancialUnlocked ? "إخفاء البيانات" : "عرض البيانات المالية";

    // ─── خصائص محسوبة للرسوم ─────────────────────────────────
    /// <summary>عرض شريط التحصيل اليومي بالبكسل — max = 180</summary>
    public double CollectionRateTodayWidth
        => (double)Math.Min(CollectionRateToday, 100) / 100.0 * 180.0;

    /// <summary>عرض شريط التحصيل الشهري بالبكسل — max = 180</summary>
    public double CollectionRateMonthWidth
        => (double)Math.Min(CollectionRateMonth, 100) / 100.0 * 180.0;

    /// <summary>هل توجد أكياس ستنتهي صلاحيتها خلال أسبوع؟</summary>
    public bool HasExpiringBags => ExpiringBags > 0;

    // ─── Partial change handlers ─────────────────────────────
    partial void OnIsFinancialUnlockedChanged(bool value)
    {
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(LockIcon));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(LockButtonText));
    }

    partial void OnCollectionRateTodayChanged(decimal value)
        => OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CollectionRateTodayWidth));

    partial void OnCollectionRateMonthChanged(decimal value)
        => OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CollectionRateMonthWidth));

    partial void OnExpiringBagsChanged(int value)
        => OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasExpiringBags));

    // ─── Constructor ─────────────────────────────────────────
    public DashboardViewModel() { _ = LoadAsync(); }

    // ─── Load ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private async Task LoadAsync()
    {
        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            using var db = new AppDbContext();
            var today = DateTime.Today;
            var nextWeek = today.AddDays(7);
            var monthStart = new DateTime(today.Year, today.Month, 1);

            // ══ إحصائيات اليوم ════════════════════════════════

            TodaySales = await db.Invoices
                .Where(i => i.Date.Date == today && !i.IsCancelled)
                .SumAsync(i => (decimal?)i.TotalAmount) ?? 0;

            // إيرادات = PaidAmount من نقدي/جزئي + سندات قبض
            TodayRevenue = await db.Invoices
                .Where(i => i.Date.Date == today && !i.IsCancelled
                         && i.PaymentType != PaymentType.Credit)
                .SumAsync(i => (decimal?)i.PaidAmount) ?? 0;
            TodayRevenue += await db.ReceiptVouchers
                .Where(v => v.Date.Date == today && v.Type == VoucherType.Receipt)
                .SumAsync(v => (decimal?)v.Amount) ?? 0;

            TodayInvoicesCount = await db.Invoices
                .CountAsync(i => i.Date.Date == today && !i.IsCancelled);

            // تصنيف الفواتير: نقدي/جزئي (محصّل) vs آجل (لم يُحصَّل)
            TodayCashInvoices = await db.Invoices.CountAsync(i =>
                i.Date.Date == today && !i.IsCancelled &&
                (i.PaymentType == PaymentType.Cash || i.PaymentType == PaymentType.Partial));

            TodayCreditInvoices = await db.Invoices.CountAsync(i =>
                i.Date.Date == today && !i.IsCancelled &&
                i.PaymentType == PaymentType.Credit);

            CollectionRateToday = TodaySales > 0
                ? Math.Round(TodayRevenue / TodaySales * 100, 1)
                : 0;

            // ══ إحصائيات الشهر ════════════════════════════════

            MonthSales = await db.Invoices
                .Where(i => i.Date >= monthStart && !i.IsCancelled)
                .SumAsync(i => (decimal?)i.TotalAmount) ?? 0;

            MonthRevenue = await db.Invoices
                .Where(i => i.Date >= monthStart && !i.IsCancelled
                         && i.PaymentType != PaymentType.Credit)
                .SumAsync(i => (decimal?)i.PaidAmount) ?? 0;
            MonthRevenue += await db.ReceiptVouchers
                .Where(v => v.Date >= monthStart && v.Type == VoucherType.Receipt)
                .SumAsync(v => (decimal?)v.Amount) ?? 0;

            MonthInvoicesCount = await db.Invoices
                .CountAsync(i => i.Date >= monthStart && !i.IsCancelled);

            CollectionRateMonth = MonthSales > 0
                ? Math.Round(MonthRevenue / MonthSales * 100, 1)
                : 0;

            // متوسط يومي = مجموع مبيعات الشهر ÷ عدد الأيام المنقضية
            int daysElapsed = Math.Max(today.Day, 1);
            AverageDailySales = Math.Round(MonthSales / daysElapsed, 0);

            // ══ مؤشرات مستمرة ════════════════════════════════

            TotalDebt = await db.Customers.SumAsync(c => (decimal?)c.Balance) ?? 0;
            SafeBalance = await db.Safes.SumAsync(s => (decimal?)s.Balance) ?? 0;
            AvailableBags = await db.BloodBags.CountAsync(b => b.Status == BloodBagStatus.Available);
            ExpiringBags = await db.BloodBags.CountAsync(b =>
                b.Status == BloodBagStatus.Available && b.ExpiryDate <= nextWeek);

            var topDebtor = await db.Customers
                .Where(c => c.Balance > 0)
                .OrderByDescending(c => c.Balance)
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync();
            TopDebtorName = topDebtor?.Name ?? "لا توجد ديون";
            TopDebtorBalance = topDebtor?.Balance ?? 0;

            // ══ رسم مبيعات آخر 7 أيام ════════════════════════

            var weekStart = today.AddDays(-6);
            var rawSales = await db.Invoices
                .Where(i => i.Date.Date >= weekStart && i.Date.Date <= today && !i.IsCancelled)
                .GroupBy(i => i.Date.Date)
                .Select(g => new
                {
                    Date = g.Key,
                    Sales = g.Sum(i => i.TotalAmount),
                    Revenue = g.Sum(i => i.PaidAmount)
                })
                .ToListAsync();

            const double MaxBarH = 110.0;
            const double MinBarH = 4.0;

            var maxSalesVal = rawSales.Any() ? (double)rawSales.Max(d => d.Sales) : 1.0;
            if (maxSalesVal == 0) maxSalesVal = 1;

            var weekPoints = new List<DailySalesPoint>();
            for (int d = 6; d >= 0; d--)
            {
                var date = today.AddDays(-d);
                var data = rawSales.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Date == date);
                var sVal = (double)(data?.Sales ?? 0);
                var rVal = (double)(data?.Revenue ?? 0);

                double sH = sVal > 0 ? Math.Max(MinBarH, sVal / maxSalesVal * MaxBarH) : 0;
                double rH = rVal > 0 ? Math.Max(MinBarH, rVal / maxSalesVal * MaxBarH) : 0;

                weekPoints.Add(new DailySalesPoint
                {
                    Label = GetArabicDayName(date),
                    DateLabel = date.ToString("d/M"),
                    Sales = data?.Sales ?? 0,
                    Revenue = data?.Revenue ?? 0,
                    SalesBarHeight = sH,
                    SalesSpacerHeight = MaxBarH - sH,
                    RevenueBarHeight = rH,
                    RevenueSpacerHeight = MaxBarH - rH,
                    IsToday = date == today
                });
            }
            WeeklySales.Clear();
            weekPoints.ForEach(WeeklySales.Add);

            // ══ توزيع فصائل الدم ══════════════════════════════

            var bagGroups = await db.BloodBags
                .Where(b => b.Status == BloodBagStatus.Available)
                .GroupBy(b => b.BloodGroup)
                .Select(g => new { Group = g.Key, Count = g.Count() })
                .ToListAsync();

            var groupColors = new Dictionary<BloodGroup, string>
            {
                { BloodGroup.APositive,  "#C0392B" }, { BloodGroup.ANegative,  "#922B21" },
                { BloodGroup.BPositive,  "#2471A3" }, { BloodGroup.BNegative,  "#1A5276" },
                { BloodGroup.ABPositive, "#1E8449" }, { BloodGroup.ABNegative, "#196F3D" },
                { BloodGroup.OPositive,  "#D35400" }, { BloodGroup.ONegative,  "#BA4A00" },
            };

            int maxCount = bagGroups.Any() ? bagGroups.Max(g => g.Count) : 1;
            if (maxCount == 0) maxCount = 1;

            BloodGroupStats.Clear();
            foreach (var bg in bagGroups.OrderByDescending(g => g.Count))
            {
                var hex = groupColors.GetValueOrDefault(bg.Group, "#666666");
                var brushColor = (System.Windows.Media.Color)
                    System.Windows.Media.ColorConverter.ConvertFromString(hex);

                BloodGroupStats.Add(new BloodGroupItem
                {
                    GroupName = bg.Group switch
                    {
                        BloodGroup.APositive => "A+",
                        BloodGroup.ANegative => "A-",
                        BloodGroup.BPositive => "B+",
                        BloodGroup.BNegative => "B-",
                        BloodGroup.ABPositive => "AB+",
                        BloodGroup.ABNegative => "AB-",
                        BloodGroup.OPositive => "O+",
                        BloodGroup.ONegative => "O-",
                        _ => bg.Group.ToString()
                    },
                    Count = bg.Count,
                    BarWidth = (double)bg.Count / maxCount * 200.0,
                    Brush = new System.Windows.Media.SolidColorBrush(brushColor)
                });
            }

            // ══ إعدادات القفل ═════════════════════════════════
            var org = await db.OrgSettings.FirstOrDefaultAsync();
            _ownerPinHash = org?.OwnerPinHash ?? string.Empty;
            IsFinancialUnlocked = string.IsNullOrEmpty(_ownerPinHash);
        }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }

    private static string GetArabicDayName(DateTime date) => date.DayOfWeek switch
    {
        DayOfWeek.Saturday => "سبت",
        DayOfWeek.Sunday => "أحد",
        DayOfWeek.Monday => "اثن",
        DayOfWeek.Tuesday => "ثلاث",
        DayOfWeek.Wednesday => "أرب",
        DayOfWeek.Thursday => "خمس",
        DayOfWeek.Friday => "جمعة",
        _ => ""
    };

    [RelayCommand]
    private void ToggleFinancialLock()
    {
        if (IsFinancialUnlocked)
        {
            IsFinancialUnlocked = false;
            return;
        }

        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_ownerPinHash))
        {
            IsFinancialUnlocked = true;
            return;
        }

        var enteredPin = Views.PinDialog.Show(
            System.Windows.Application.Current.MainWindow);
        if (enteredPin is null) return;

        if (PinHelper.Verify(enteredPin, _ownerPinHash))
            IsFinancialUnlocked = true;
        else
            ShowError("الرمز السري غير صحيح");
    }
}
