using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using System.Windows;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels.Base;

public abstract partial class ViewModelBase : ObservableObject
{
    [ObservableProperty]
    private bool _isBusy;

    [ObservableProperty]
    private string _statusMessage = string.Empty;

    protected static void ShowError(string message)
        => MessageBox.Show(message, "خطأ", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Error);

    protected static void ShowSuccess(string message)
        => MessageBox.Show(message, "تم", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Information);

    protected static bool Confirm(string message)
        => MessageBox.Show(message, "تأكيد", MessageBoxButton.YesNo, MessageBoxImage.Question) == MessageBoxResult.Yes;
}
</file>

<file path="ViewModels/PaymentVoucherViewModel.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Helpers;
using BloodBankAccounting.Models;
using BloodBankAccounting.Printing;
using BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels.Base;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;


namespace BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels;

public partial class PaymentVoucherViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    [ObservableProperty] private string _voucherNumber = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private DateTime _date = DateTime.Today;

    /// <summary>اسم المستلم (موظف / مورّد / جهة)</summary>
    [ObservableProperty] private string _payeeName = string.Empty;

    /// <summary>البيان — وصف سبب الصرف</summary>
    [ObservableProperty] private string? _notes;

    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _amount;
    [ObservableProperty] private Safe? _selectedSafe;

    public ObservableCollection<Safe> Safes { get; } = [];

    /// <summary>رقم السند المحفوظ — يُستخدم لطباعة السند بعد الحفظ</summary>
    private int? _savedVoucherId;

    public bool CanPrint => _savedVoucherId.HasValue;

    public PaymentVoucherViewModel() { _ = LoadAsync(); }

    private async Task LoadAsync()
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var safes = await db.Safes.OrderBy(s => s.Name).ToListAsync();
        Safes.Clear(); safes.ForEach(Safes.Add);
        VoucherNumber = await NumberGenerator.NextPaymentVoucherNumberAsync(db);
        SelectedSafe = safes.FirstOrDefault(s => s.IsMain);
        _savedVoucherId = null;
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CanPrint));
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task SaveAsync()
    {
        if (!Validate()) return;

        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            using var db = new AppDbContext();
            await using var tx = await db.Database.BeginTransactionAsync();

            var voucher = new ReceiptVoucher
            {
                VoucherNumber = VoucherNumber,
                Type = VoucherType.Payment,
                Date = Date,
                SafeId = SelectedSafe!.Id,
                PayeeName = PayeeName.Trim(),
                Amount = Amount,
                Notes = Notes
            };

            db.ReceiptVouchers.Add(voucher);
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            // خصم المبلغ من الخزينة (المال يخرج)
            var safe = await db.Safes.FindAsync(SelectedSafe!.Id);
            safe!.Balance -= Amount;

            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            // قيد المحاسبة: مصاريف مدين — خزينة دائن
            JournalService.PostPayment(db, voucher);
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            await tx.CommitAsync();

            _savedVoucherId = voucher.Id;
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CanPrint));
            ShowSuccess($"تم حفظ سند الدفع {VoucherNumber} ✓");
        }
        catch (Exception ex) { ShowError(ex.Message); }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task PrintAsync()
    {
        if (!_savedVoucherId.HasValue) return;
        IsBusy = true;
        try { await PaymentPrinter.PrintAsync(_savedVoucherId.Value); }
        catch (Exception ex) { ShowError(ex.Message); }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task NewVoucherAsync()
    {
        PayeeName = string.Empty;
        Notes = null;
        Amount = 0;
        await LoadAsync();
    }

    private bool Validate()
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(PayeeName)) { ShowError("أدخل اسم المستلم"); return false; }
        if (SelectedSafe is null) { ShowError("اختر الخزينة"); return false; }
        if (Amount <= 0) { ShowError("المبلغ يجب أن يكون أكبر من صفر"); return false; }

        // تحقق من الرصيد الكافي
        if (SelectedSafe.Balance < Amount)
        {
            ShowError($"رصيد الخزينة غير كافٍ\nالرصيد الحالي: {SelectedSafe.Balance:N2} ج.م\nالمطلوب صرفه: {Amount:N2} ج.م");
            return false;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
</file>

<file path="ViewModels/SafeReportViewModel.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Helpers;
using BloodBankAccounting.Models;
using BloodBankAccounting.Printing;
using BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels.Base;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels;

public partial class SafeReportViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  القفل المالي
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    [ObservableProperty] private bool _isFinancialUnlocked = true;
    private string _ownerPinHash = string.Empty;

    public string LockIcon => IsFinancialUnlocked ? "🔓" : "🔒";
    public string LockButtonText => IsFinancialUnlocked ? "إخفاء البيانات" : "عرض البيانات المالية";

    partial void OnIsFinancialUnlockedChanged(bool value)
    {
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(LockIcon));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(LockButtonText));
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void ToggleFinancialLock()
    {
        if (IsFinancialUnlocked) { IsFinancialUnlocked = false; return; }

        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_ownerPinHash)) { IsFinancialUnlocked = true; return; }

        var enteredPin = Views.PinDialog.Show(System.Windows.Application.Current.MainWindow);
        if (enteredPin is null) return;

        if (PinHelper.Verify(enteredPin, _ownerPinHash))
            IsFinancialUnlocked = true;
        else
            ShowError("الرمز السري غير صحيح");
    }

    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  بيانات الخزينة
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    public ObservableCollection<Safe> Safes { get; } = [];

    /// <summary>جميع الحركات المحمّلة من DB (المصدر الكامل)</summary>
    private readonly ObservableCollection<SafeEntryRow> _allEntries = [];

    /// <summary>الحركات المعروضة في الجدول بعد تطبيق البحث النصي</summary>
    public ObservableCollection<SafeEntryRow> Entries { get; } = [];

    [ObservableProperty] private Safe? _selectedSafe;

    // ── النطاق الزمني — الافتراضي: آخر يوم واحد فقط ──────────────────
    [ObservableProperty] private DateTime _from = DateTime.Today;
    [ObservableProperty] private DateTime _to = DateTime.Today;

    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _openingBalance;
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _closingBalance;
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _totalDebit;
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _totalCredit;

    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  البحث النصي
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    [ObservableProperty] private string _searchText = string.Empty;

    /// <summary>عدد النتائج بعد الفلترة — يُعرض في الـ UI</summary>
    public int FilteredCount => Entries.Count;

    partial void OnSearchTextChanged(string value) => ApplySearch();

    /// <summary>
    /// يُفلتر _allEntries بناءً على SearchText ويُحدّث Entries.
    /// البحث يشمل: التاريخ، النوع، البيان، المبالغ.
    /// </summary>
    private void ApplySearch()
    {
        var term = SearchText?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;

        Entries.Clear();

        var filtered = string.IsNullOrEmpty(term)
            ? _allEntries
            : _allEntries.Where(e =>
                e.Date.ToString("d/M/yyyy").Contains(term) ||
                e.RefTypeDisplay.Contains(term, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                e.Description.Contains(term, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                e.Debit.ToString("N2").Contains(term) ||
                e.Credit.ToString("N2").Contains(term) ||
                e.Balance.ToString("N2").Contains(term));

        foreach (var row in filtered)
            Entries.Add(row);

        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(FilteredCount));
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void ClearSearch()
    {
        SearchText = string.Empty;   // OnSearchTextChanged → ApplySearch
    }

    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  تحميل
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    public SafeReportViewModel() { _ = LoadAsync(); }

    private async Task LoadAsync()
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var safes = await db.Safes.OrderBy(s => s.Name).ToListAsync();
        Safes.Clear();
        safes.ForEach(Safes.Add);
        SelectedSafe = safes.FirstOrDefault(s => s.IsMain) ?? safes.FirstOrDefault();

        var org = await db.OrgSettings.FirstOrDefaultAsync();
        _ownerPinHash = org?.OwnerPinHash ?? string.Empty;

        IsFinancialUnlocked = string.IsNullOrEmpty(_ownerPinHash);
    }

    partial void OnSelectedSafeChanged(Safe? value)
    {
        if (value is not null) _ = LoadStatementAsync();
    }

    // ── التاريخ لا يُحمّل تلقائياً — فقط عبر زر "عرض" ──────────────────
    // (تم حذف partial void OnFromChanged / OnToChanged العتيقتين)

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task LoadStatementAsync()
    {
        if (SelectedSafe is null) return;

        using var db = new AppDbContext();

        var safeCode = SelectedSafe.Type == SafeType.Cash
            ? AccountCodes.Cash   // "1001"
            : AccountCodes.Bank;  // "1002"

        var beforeDebit = await db.JournalEntries
            .Where(j => j.Date < From && j.AccountCode == safeCode)
            .SumAsync(j => (decimal?)j.DebitAmount) ?? 0;

        var beforeCredit = await db.JournalEntries
            .Where(j => j.Date < From && j.AccountCode == safeCode)
            .SumAsync(j => (decimal?)j.CreditAmount) ?? 0;

        OpeningBalance = beforeDebit - beforeCredit;

        var entries = await db.JournalEntries
            .Where(j => j.Date >= From && j.Date <= To && j.AccountCode == safeCode)
            .OrderBy(j => j.Date)
            .ToListAsync();

        _allEntries.Clear();
        var runningBalance = OpeningBalance;

        foreach (var e in entries)
        {
            runningBalance += e.DebitAmount - e.CreditAmount;
            _allEntries.Add(new SafeEntryRow
            {
                Date = e.Date,
                RefType = e.RefType,
                RefId = e.RefId,
                RefTypeDisplay = e.RefType switch
                {
                    "Invoice" => "فاتورة",
                    "Receipt" => "قبض",
                    "Payment" => "صرف",
                    _ => e.RefType
                },
                Description = e.AccountName,
                Debit = e.DebitAmount,
                Credit = e.CreditAmount,
                Balance = runningBalance
            });
        }

        TotalDebit = entries.Sum(e => e.DebitAmount);
        TotalCredit = entries.Sum(e => e.CreditAmount);
        ClosingBalance = runningBalance;

        // تطبيق أي بحث موجود على البيانات الجديدة
        ApplySearch();
    }

    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  طباعة الكشف كله
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task PrintAsync()
    {
        if (SelectedSafe is null) { ShowError("اختر الخزينة أولاً"); return; }
        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            await SafeStatementPrinter.PrintAsync(
                SelectedSafe.Name,
                From, To,
                OpeningBalance, ClosingBalance,
                TotalDebit, TotalCredit,
                Entries.ToList());
        }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }

    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  طباعة حركة واحدة بعينها
    // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task PrintEntryAsync(SafeEntryRow entry)
    {
        if (entry is null) return;
        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            switch (entry.RefType)
            {
                case "Invoice":
                    await InvoicePrinter.PrintAsync(entry.RefId);
                    break;
                case "Receipt":
                    await ReceiptPrinter.PrintAsync(entry.RefId);
                    break;
                case "Payment":
                    await PaymentPrinter.PrintAsync(entry.RefId);
                    break;
                default:
                    ShowError($"لا توجد طباعة متاحة لهذا النوع: {entry.RefTypeDisplay}");
                    break;
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex) { ShowError($"خطأ في الطباعة:\n{ex.Message}"); }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  صف كشف الحساب
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
public class SafeEntryRow
{
    public DateTime Date { get; set; }
    public string RefType { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public int RefId { get; set; }
    public string RefTypeDisplay { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public string Description { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public decimal Debit { get; set; }
    public decimal Credit { get; set; }
    public decimal Balance { get; set; }
}

using BloodBankAccounting.Helpers;
using BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels.Base;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Threading;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels;

public partial class TrialViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    [ObservableProperty] private string _activationKey = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _machineId;
    [ObservableProperty] private bool _hasError;

    // FIX: اسم مختلف عن StatusMessage في ViewModelBase لتفادي التعارض
    [ObservableProperty] private string _trialStatus = string.Empty;

    // FIX: نص زر النسخ — بيتغير لحظة الضغط ويرجع تاني بعد ثانيتين
    [ObservableProperty] private string _copyButtonText = "📋 نسخ";

    public int DaysRemaining { get; } = TrialManager.DaysRemaining();
    public bool IsExpired { get; } = TrialManager.DaysRemaining() == 0;

    public event Action? ActivationSucceeded;

    private readonly DispatcherTimer _copyResetTimer;

    public TrialViewModel()
    {
        _machineId = TrialManager.GetMachineId();

        _copyResetTimer = new DispatcherTimer
        {
            Interval = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(2)
        };
        _copyResetTimer.Tick += (_, _) =>
        {
            CopyButtonText = "📋 نسخ";
            TrialStatus = string.Empty;
            _copyResetTimer.Stop();
        };
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void Activate()
    {
        HasError = false;
        TrialStatus = string.Empty;

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(ActivationKey))
        {
            HasError = true;
            TrialStatus = "أدخل كود التفعيل";
            return;
        }

        if (TrialManager.Activate(ActivationKey))
        {
            MessageBox.Show(
                "✅ تم تفعيل البرنامج بنجاح!\nشكراً لثقتكم في شريان.",
                "تم التفعيل",
                MessageBoxButton.OK,
                MessageBoxImage.Information);

            ActivationSucceeded?.Invoke();
        }
        else
        {
            HasError = true;
            TrialStatus = "كود التفعيل غير صحيح. تأكد من الكود وحاول مجدداً.";
        }
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void CopyMachineId()
    {
        Clipboard.SetText(MachineId);

        // FIX: تغيير فوري لزر النسخ + رسالة نجاح خضراء
        CopyButtonText = "✅ تم";
        TrialStatus = "✓ تم نسخ معرّف الجهاز";
        HasError = false;

        _copyResetTimer.Stop();
        _copyResetTimer.Start();
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void ContinueTrial()
    {
        ActivationSucceeded?.Invoke();
    }
}

<file path="Views/AdminPasswordDialog.xaml.cs">
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Input;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Views;

public partial class AdminPasswordDialog : Window
{
    // SHA-256 لكلمة المرور: kadkksjfkaj;ajkfjdkaj;j
    // لتغيير الكلمة: شغّل → python3 -c "import hashlib; print(hashlib.sha256('كلمتك'.encode()).hexdigest())"
    private const string CorrectHash = "3f8e20a6f47525989ccec1c73cfc0e0733863cc6abbbcdce7d146f68095a96cf";

//    لو عايز تغيّر كلمة السر مستقبلاً
//python3 -c "import hashlib; print(hashlib.sha256('كلمتك_الجديدة'.encode()).hexdigest())"
    public bool Authenticated { get; private set; }

    public AdminPasswordDialog() => InitializeComponent();

    private void BtnConfirm_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e) => TryAuthenticate();
    private void BtnCancel_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e) => Close();

    private void PwdBox_KeyDown(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
    {
        if (e.Key == Key.Enter) TryAuthenticate();
        if (e.Key == Key.Escape) Close();
    }

    private void TryAuthenticate()
    {
        var input = PwdBox.Password;
        var hash = Convert.ToHexString(
            SHA256.HashData(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(input))).ToLower();

        if (hash == CorrectHash)
        {
            Authenticated = true;
            Close();
        }
        else
        {
            TxtError.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
            PwdBox.Clear();
            PwdBox.Focus();

            // اهزّ النافذة
            ShakeWindow();
        }
    }

    // تأثير اهتزاز بسيط عند الخطأ
    private async void ShakeWindow()
    {
        var original = Left;
        int[] offsets = [10, -10, 8, -8, 5, -5, 0];
        foreach (var offset in offsets)
        {
            Left = original + offset;
            await Task.Delay(40);
        }
        Left = original;
    }
}
</file>

using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Media;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Views;

public partial class BloodInventoryView : UserControl
{
    public BloodInventoryView()
    {
        InitializeComponent();

        // ??? ????? ??????? ???????? ??? ????? ?????? ????????
        // ????? ??? ???? ???????? ??? ??? ????? ???? ??????
        // ???????? ????? ?????? ?????? ?????? ??? ?? ???.
        Loaded += (_, _) =>
        {
            TxtBagNumber.Focus();
            ActivateScanBorder();
        };

        TxtBagNumber.GotFocus += (_, _) => ActivateScanBorder();
        TxtBagNumber.LostFocus += (_, _) => DeactivateScanBorder();
    }

    // ??? ??? ?????? ???????? ? ????? ????? ?????????????????
    private void ScanIconBorder_Click(object sender, System.Windows.Input.MouseButtonEventArgs e)
    {
        TxtBagNumber.Focus();
        TxtBagNumber.SelectAll();
    }

    // ??? ????? ??? ?????? ??? ??????? ??????????????????????
    private void ActivateScanBorder()
    {
        BarcodeFieldBorder.BorderBrush =
            new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0xC0, 0x39, 0x2B)); // PrimaryBrush
        BarcodeFieldBorder.BorderThickness = new Thickness(2);
        ScanIconBorder.Background =
            new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0xC0, 0x39, 0x2B));
    }

    private void DeactivateScanBorder()
    {
        BarcodeFieldBorder.BorderBrush =
            new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0xE5, 0xE8, 0xEC));
        BarcodeFieldBorder.BorderThickness = new Thickness(1.5);
        ScanIconBorder.Background =
            new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0x1A, 0x27, 0x44));
    }
}

<file path="Views/InvoiceView.xaml.cs">
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Media;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Views;

public partial class InvoiceView : UserControl
{
    public InvoiceView()
    {
        InitializeComponent();

        // ??? ????? ??? ????? ??? ????? ???? ???????? ????????
        Loaded += (_, _) =>
        {
            TxtScanInvoice.Focus();
            ActivateInvoiceScanBorder();
        };

        TxtScanInvoice.GotFocus += (_, _) => ActivateInvoiceScanBorder();
        TxtScanInvoice.LostFocus += (_, _) => DeactivateInvoiceScanBorder();
    }

    // ??? ??? ?????? ???????? ????????????????????????????????
    private void InvoiceScanIcon_Click(object sender,
        System.Windows.Input.MouseButtonEventArgs e)
    {
        TxtScanInvoice.Focus();
        TxtScanInvoice.SelectAll();
    }

    private void ActivateInvoiceScanBorder()
    {
        InvoiceScanBorder.BorderBrush =
            new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0xC0, 0x39, 0x2B));
        InvoiceScanBorder.BorderThickness = new Thickness(2);
        InvoiceScanIcon.Background =
            new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0xC0, 0x39, 0x2B));
    }

    private void DeactivateInvoiceScanBorder()
    {
        InvoiceScanBorder.BorderBrush =
            new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0xE5, 0xE8, 0xEC));
        InvoiceScanBorder.BorderThickness = new Thickness(1.5);
        InvoiceScanIcon.Background =
            new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0x1A, 0x27, 0x44));
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Views/OrganizationSettingsView.xaml.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Views;

public partial class OrganizationSettingsView : UserControl
{
    public OrganizationSettingsView() => InitializeComponent();

    // PasswordBox لا يدعم Binding مباشر لأسباب أمنية — نمرر القيمة عبر Code-behind
    private void OnChangePinClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (DataContext is not OrgSettingsViewModel vm) return;
        vm.ChangePinFromView(CurrentPinBox.Password, NewPinBox.Password);
        CurrentPinBox.Clear();
        NewPinBox.Clear();
    }

    private void OnRemovePinClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (DataContext is not OrgSettingsViewModel vm) return;
        vm.RemovePinFromView(CurrentPinBox.Password);
        CurrentPinBox.Clear();
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Views/PinDialog.xaml.cs">
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Input;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Views;

public partial class PinDialog : Window
{
    public string EnteredPin { get; private set; } = string.Empty;

    public PinDialog()
    {
        InitializeComponent();
        OkBtn.Click += (_, _) => Confirm();
        CancelBtn.Click += (_, _) => { DialogResult = false; Close(); };
        PinBox.KeyDown += (_, e) => { if (e.Key == Key.Return) Confirm(); };
        Loaded += (_, _) => PinBox.Focus();

        // السحب من الشريط الفارغ
        MouseLeftButtonDown += (_, e) => { if (e.ButtonState == MouseButtonState.Pressed) DragMove(); };
    }

    private void Confirm()
    {
        ErrorText.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;   // Hidden لا Collapsed للحفاظ على المساحة
        EnteredPin = PinBox.Password;
        DialogResult = true;
        Close();
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// عرض الحوار وإرجاع الرمز المُدخَل، أو null عند الإلغاء.
    /// </summary>
    public static string? Show(Window? owner = null)
    {
        var dlg = new PinDialog();
        if (owner is not null) dlg.Owner = owner;
        return dlg.ShowDialog() == true ? dlg.EnteredPin : null;
    }

    /// <summary>يُظهر رسالة الخطأ ويمسح الحقل (للاستخدام الخارجي)</summary>
    public void ShowError()
    {
        ErrorText.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
        PinBox.Clear();
        PinBox.Focus();
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Views/SecretAdminPanel.xaml.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using System.Windows;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Views;

public partial class SecretAdminPanel : Window
{
    public SecretAdminPanel()
    {
        InitializeComponent();
    }

    // ─── Backup ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private async void BtnBackup_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        SetStatus("جاري إنشاء النسخة الاحتياطية...", "#F39C12");
        BtnBackup.IsEnabled = false;

        try
        {
            var path = await DatabaseAdminService.BackupDatabaseAsync();
            SetStatus($"✓ تم الحفظ في:\n{path}", "#2ECC71");
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            SetStatus($"✗ فشل: {ex.Message}", "#E74C3C");
        }
        finally
        {
            BtnBackup.IsEnabled = true;
        }
    }

    // ─── Clear Data ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private async void BtnClear_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        var confirm = MessageBox.Show(
            "سيتم حذف كل البيانات نهائياً.\nهل أنت متأكد؟",
            "تأكيد المسح",
            MessageBoxButton.YesNo,
            MessageBoxImage.Warning);

        if (confirm != MessageBoxResult.Yes) return;

        SetStatus("جاري مسح البيانات...", "#F39C12");
        BtnClear.IsEnabled = false;

        try
        {
            await DatabaseAdminService.ClearAllDataAsync();
            SetStatus("✓ تم مسح كل البيانات بنجاح.", "#2ECC71");
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            SetStatus($"✗ فشل: {ex.Message}", "#E74C3C");
        }
        finally
        {
            BtnClear.IsEnabled = true;
        }
    }

    // ─── Drop Database ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private async void BtnDrop_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        // تأكيد مزدوج لأن العملية لا رجعة منها
        var first = MessageBox.Show(
            "⚠ تحذير: سيتم حذف قاعدة البيانات بالكامل من الـ SQL Server.\n" +
            "هذه العملية لا يمكن التراجع عنها.\n\nهل تريد المتابعة؟",
            "حذف قاعدة البيانات",
            MessageBoxButton.YesNo,
            MessageBoxImage.Warning);

        if (first != MessageBoxResult.Yes) return;

        var second = MessageBox.Show(
            "تأكيد أخير: هل أنت متأكد 100% من حذف قاعدة البيانات؟",
            "تأكيد نهائي",
            MessageBoxButton.YesNo,
            MessageBoxImage.Stop);

        if (second != MessageBoxResult.Yes) return;

        SetStatus("جاري الحذف...", "#F39C12");
        BtnDrop.IsEnabled = false;

        try
        {
            await DatabaseAdminService.DropDatabaseAsync();
            SetStatus("✓ تم حذف قاعدة البيانات. أغلق التطبيق الآن.", "#2ECC71");

            // إغلاق التطبيق بعد 2 ثانية
            await Task.Delay(2000);
            Application.Current.Shutdown();
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            SetStatus($"✗ فشل: {ex.Message}", "#E74C3C");
            BtnDrop.IsEnabled = true;
        }
    }

    private void SetStatus(string msg, string color)
    {
        TxtStatus.Text = msg;
        TxtStatus.Foreground = new System.Windows.Media.BrushConverter()
            .ConvertFromString(color) as System.Windows.Media.Brush;
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Views/Styles.xaml">
<ResourceDictionary xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml/presentation"
                    xmlns:x="http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml"
                    xmlns:converters="clr-namespace:BloodBankAccounting.Converters">

    <!--
        IsEqualConverter مُعرَّف هنا (وليس في App.xaml) لأنه يُستخدم
        داخل NavButton triggers — لازم يكون في نفس الـ ResourceDictionary.
    -->
    <converters:IsEqualConverter x:Key="IsEqualConverter"/>

    <!-- ═══════════ COLORS ═══════════════════════════════════ -->
    <SolidColorBrush x:Key="PrimaryBrush"       Color="#C0392B"/>
    <SolidColorBrush x:Key="PrimaryDarkBrush"   Color="#922B21"/>
    <SolidColorBrush x:Key="SurfaceBrush"       Color="#FFFFFF"/>
    <SolidColorBrush x:Key="BackgroundBrush"    Color="#F4F6F8"/>
    <SolidColorBrush x:Key="SidebarBrush"       Color="#1A2744"/>
    <SolidColorBrush x:Key="TextBrush"          Color="#1C2833"/>
    <SolidColorBrush x:Key="TextSecondaryBrush" Color="#566573"/>
    <SolidColorBrush x:Key="BorderBrush"        Color="#E5E8EC"/>
    <SolidColorBrush x:Key="SuccessBrush"       Color="#27AE60"/>
    <SolidColorBrush x:Key="WarningBrush"       Color="#F39C12"/>

    <!-- ═══════════ NAV BUTTON ════════════════════════════════
         الإصلاحات:
           • FocusVisualStyle=Null   → يزيل الإطار الأصفر عند الضغط بـ Tab
           • Hover: #2A3F6F solid    → واضح ومريح للعين
           • Active DataTrigger      → أحمر لما الصفحة مفتوحة
    ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ -->
    <Style x:Key="NavButton" TargetType="Button">
        <Setter Property="Background"              Value="Transparent"/>
        <Setter Property="Foreground"              Value="#AAAAAA"/>
        <Setter Property="BorderThickness"         Value="0"/>
        <Setter Property="Padding"                 Value="10,9"/>
        <Setter Property="Margin"                  Value="0,2"/>
        <Setter Property="FontSize"                Value="13.5"/>
        <Setter Property="FontFamily"              Value="Segoe UI, Tahoma, Arial"/>
        <Setter Property="HorizontalContentAlignment" Value="Right"/>
        <Setter Property="Cursor"                  Value="Hand"/>
        <!-- ✦ يُزيل الإطار الأصفر المزعج عند التنقل بالـ Tab -->
        <Setter Property="FocusVisualStyle"        Value="{x:Null}"/>
        <Setter Property="Template">
            <Setter.Value>
                <ControlTemplate TargetType="Button">
                    <Border x:Name="border" CornerRadius="8"
                            Background="{TemplateBinding Background}"
                            Padding="{TemplateBinding Padding}">
                        <ContentPresenter HorizontalAlignment="{TemplateBinding HorizontalContentAlignment}"/>
                    </Border>
                    <ControlTemplate.Triggers>

                        <!-- ① Hover: لون سيدبار أفتح قليلاً + نص أبيض -->
                        <Trigger Property="IsMouseOver" Value="True">
                            <Setter TargetName="border" Property="Background" Value="#2A3F6F"/>
                            <Setter Property="Foreground" Value="White"/>
                        </Trigger>

                        <!-- ② Pressed -->
                        <Trigger Property="IsPressed" Value="True">
                            <Setter TargetName="border" Property="Background" Value="#C0392B88"/>
                            <Setter Property="Foreground" Value="White"/>
                        </Trigger>

                        <!--
                            ③ Active — الصفحة المفتوحة حالياً
                            يقارن Tag الزر بـ ActivePage في الـ ViewModel.
                            يكون آخر trigger علشان يفضل ظاهر حتى لو الماوس عليه.
                        -->
                        <DataTrigger Value="True">
                            <DataTrigger.Binding>
                                <MultiBinding Converter="{StaticResource IsEqualConverter}">
                                    <Binding Path="DataContext.ActivePage"
                                             RelativeSource="{RelativeSource AncestorType=Window}"/>
                                    <Binding Path="Tag"
                                             RelativeSource="{RelativeSource TemplatedParent}"/>
                                </MultiBinding>
                            </DataTrigger.Binding>
                            <Setter TargetName="border" Property="Background" Value="#C0392B"/>
                            <Setter Property="Foreground"                     Value="White"/>
                            <Setter Property="FontWeight"                     Value="SemiBold"/>
                        </DataTrigger>

                    </ControlTemplate.Triggers>
                </ControlTemplate>
            </Setter.Value>
        </Setter>
    </Style>

    <!-- ═══════════ PRIMARY BUTTON ═══════════════════════════ -->
    <Style x:Key="PrimaryButton" TargetType="Button">
        <Setter Property="Background"      Value="#C0392B"/>

<Window x:Class="BloodBankAccounting.Views.TrialView"
        xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml/presentation"
        xmlns:x="http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml"
        Title="شريان — تفعيل البرنامج"

        ResizeMode="NoResize"

                BorderBrush="#E5E8EC" BorderThickness="1">

<file path="Views/TrialView.xaml.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels;
using System.Windows;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Views;

public partial class TrialView : Window
{
    public bool Proceed { get; private set; }

    public TrialView()
    {
        InitializeComponent();
        var vm = new TrialViewModel();
        vm.ActivationSucceeded += () => { Proceed = true; Close(); };
        DataContext = vm;
    }
}
</file>

<file path="App.xaml.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Helpers;
using BloodBankAccounting.Views;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using QuestPDF.Infrastructure;
using System.Windows;

namespace BloodBankAccounting;

public partial class App : Application
{
    protected override async void OnStartup(StartupEventArgs e)
    {
        base.OnStartup(e);

        ShutdownMode = ShutdownMode.OnExplicitShutdown;

        QuestPDF.Settings.License = LicenseType.Community;

        // ─── 1. Trial / License check ──────────────────────────────────
        if (!TrialManager.IsActivated())
        {
            var trial = new TrialView();
            trial.ShowDialog();
            if (!trial.Proceed) { Shutdown(); return; }
        }

        // ─── 2. Database ───────────────────────────────────────────────
        try
        {
            using var db = new AppDbContext();
            await db.Database.MigrateAsync();
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            MessageBox.Show(
                $"فشل الاتصال بقاعدة البيانات:\n{ex.Message}",
                "خطأ في الاتصال",
                MessageBoxButton.OK,
                MessageBoxImage.Error);
            Shutdown();
            return;
        }

        // ─── 3. Open Main Window ───────────────────────────────────────
        var main = new MainWindow();
        MainWindow = main;
        ShutdownMode = ShutdownMode.OnMainWindowClose;
        main.Show();

        // ─── 4. بدء النسخ الاحتياطي التلقائي ─────────────────────────
        // يبدأ بعد ما الـ MainWindow يتفتح حتى لا يؤثر على وقت التحميل.
        // يعمل في الخلفية — لا يوقف واجهة المستخدم.
        AutoBackupService.Instance.BackupFailed += OnAutoBackupFailed;
        AutoBackupService.Instance.Start();
    }

    // ─── عرض إشعار صامت لو فشل الـ Backup (اختياري) ──────────────────
    private void OnAutoBackupFailed(Exception ex)
    {
        // نُبلّغ على الـ UI Thread
        Dispatcher.InvokeAsync(() =>
        {
            // رسالة خفيفة في الـ Title Bar بدل MessageBox مزعجة
            if (MainWindow is not null)
                MainWindow.Title = "بنك الدم — نظام المحاسبة  ⚠ فشل النسخ الاحتياطي";
        });
    }

    protected override void OnExit(ExitEventArgs e)
    {
        AutoBackupService.Instance.Dispose();
        base.OnExit(e);
    }
}
</file>

<file path="ViewModels/InvoiceViewModel.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Helpers;
using BloodBankAccounting.Models;
using BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels.Base;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels;

public partial class InvoiceViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    // ─── Header ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    [ObservableProperty] private string _invoiceNumber = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private DateTime _date = DateTime.Today;

    [ObservableProperty]
    [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(IsSafeVisible))]
    [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(IsPartialVisible))]
    [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(RemainingAfterInitial))]
    private PaymentType _paymentType = PaymentType.Cash;

    [ObservableProperty] private Customer? _selectedCustomer;
    [ObservableProperty] private Safe? _selectedSafe;

    [ObservableProperty]
    [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(RemainingAfterInitial))]
    private decimal _initialPayment;

    // ─── Quick Add Customer ───────────────────────────────────
    [ObservableProperty] private bool _isQuickAddVisible;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _newCustomerName = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private CustomerType _newCustomerType = CustomerType.Hospital;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _newCustomerPhone = string.Empty;

    public IEnumerable<CustomerType> CustomerTypes { get; } = Enum.GetValues<CustomerType>();

    // ─── Manual Line Entry ────────────────────────────────────
    [ObservableProperty]
    [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(NewLineTotal))]
    private string _newLineName = string.Empty;

    [ObservableProperty]
    [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(NewLineTotal))]
    private int _newLineQty = 1;

    [ObservableProperty]
    [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(NewLineTotal))]
    private decimal _newLineUnitPrice;

    public decimal NewLineTotal => NewLineQty * NewLineUnitPrice;

    // ─── Barcode Scan (secondary) ─────────────────────────────
    [ObservableProperty] private string _scanBagNumber = string.Empty;

    // ─── Lines ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    public ObservableCollection<InvoiceLineItem> Lines { get; } = [];

    // ─── Totals ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _discountAmount;
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _subTotal;
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _totalAmount;

    public bool IsSafeVisible => PaymentType == PaymentType.Cash || PaymentType == PaymentType.Partial;
    public bool IsPartialVisible => PaymentType == PaymentType.Partial;
    public decimal RemainingAfterInitial => Math.Max(0, TotalAmount - InitialPayment);

    // ─── Lookup lists ─────────────────────────────────────────
    public ObservableCollection<Customer> Customers { get; } = [];
    public ObservableCollection<Safe> Safes { get; } = [];
    public ObservableCollection<BloodBag> AvailableBags { get; } = [];

    public IEnumerable<PaymentType> PaymentTypes { get; } = Enum.GetValues<PaymentType>();

    /// <summary>أنواع منتجات الدم الشائعة — قابلة للتحرير الحر في ComboBox</summary>
    public IReadOnlyList<string> BloodProductTypes { get; } =
    [
        "دم كامل",
        "كرات دم مكدسه",
        "بلازما طازجه مجمده",
        "كرايو - راسب اببض",
        "٦ وحدات صفائح دموية",
        " وحدة صفائح",
        "فلترة كيس دم",
        "غسيل كيس دم",
        "كيس دم اطفال",
        "كرات دم مكدسه موجب",
        "كرات دم مكدسه سالب"
    ];

    public InvoiceViewModel() { _ = LoadAsync(); }

    private async Task LoadAsync()
    {
        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            using var db = new AppDbContext();

            var customers = await db.Customers.Where(c => c.IsActive).OrderBy(c => c.Name).ToListAsync();
            var safes = await db.Safes.OrderBy(s => s.Name).ToListAsync();
            var bags = await db.BloodBags
                .Where(b => b.Status == BloodBagStatus.Available && b.ExpiryDate > DateTime.Today)
                .OrderBy(b => b.ExpiryDate)
                .ToListAsync();

            Customers.Clear(); customers.ForEach(Customers.Add);
            Safes.Clear(); safes.ForEach(Safes.Add);
            AvailableBags.Clear(); bags.ForEach(AvailableBags.Add);

            InvoiceNumber = await NumberGenerator.NextInvoiceNumberAsync(db);
            SelectedSafe = safes.FirstOrDefault(s => s.IsMain);
        }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }

    // ─── Quick Add Customer ───────────────────────────────────
    [RelayCommand]
    private void ToggleQuickAdd()
    {
        IsQuickAddVisible = !IsQuickAddVisible;
        NewCustomerName = string.Empty;
        NewCustomerPhone = string.Empty;
        NewCustomerType = CustomerType.Hospital;
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task QuickSaveCustomerAsync()
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(NewCustomerName))
        {
            ShowError("أدخل اسم العميل");
            return;
        }

        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            using var db = new AppDbContext();

            var customer = new Customer
            {
                Name = NewCustomerName.Trim(),
                Type = NewCustomerType,
                Phone = NewCustomerPhone.Trim(),
                IsActive = true
            };

            db.Customers.Add(customer);
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            Customers.Add(customer);
            SelectedCustomer = customer;
            IsQuickAddVisible = false;
            NewCustomerName = string.Empty;
            NewCustomerPhone = string.Empty;
            ShowSuccess($"تمت إضافة العميل «{customer.Name}» ✓");
        }
        catch (Exception ex) { ShowError(ex.Message); }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }

    // ─── إضافة صنف يدوي ──────────────────────────────────────
    [RelayCommand]
    private void AddManualLine()
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(NewLineName))
        {
            ShowError("أدخل اسم الصنف"); return;
        }
        if (NewLineQty <= 0)
        {
            ShowError("الكمية يجب أن تكون أكبر من صفر"); return;
        }
        if (NewLineUnitPrice <= 0)
        {
            ShowError("أدخل سعر الوحدة"); return;
        }

        var line = new InvoiceLineItem
        {
            BloodBagId = null,
            IsManual = true,
            ProductName = NewLineName.Trim(),
            UnitPrice = NewLineUnitPrice,
            Quantity = NewLineQty
        };
        line.PropertyChanged += (_, _) => RecalculateTotals();
        Lines.Add(line);
        RecalculateTotals();

        // إعادة تعيين حقول الإدخال
        NewLineName = string.Empty;
        NewLineQty = 1;
        NewLineUnitPrice = 0;
    }

    // ─── إضافة كيس بالنقر ────────────────────────────────────
    [RelayCommand]
    private void AddLine(BloodBag bag)
    {
        if (Lines.Any(l => l.BloodBagId == bag.Id))
        {
            ShowError("هذا الكيس مضاف بالفعل"); return;
        }

        var line = new InvoiceLineItem
        {
            BloodBagId = bag.Id,
            IsManual = false,
            ProductName = $"{bag.BagNumber} — {bag.BloodGroup.ToArabic()}",
            UnitPrice = bag.SalePrice,
            Quantity = 1
        };
        line.PropertyChanged += (_, _) => RecalculateTotals();
        Lines.Add(line);
        RecalculateTotals();
    }

    // ─── إضافة كيس بالباركود ─────────────────────────────────
    [RelayCommand]
    private void AddLineByBarcode()
    {
        var barcode = ScanBagNumber.Trim();
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(barcode)) return;

        var bag = AvailableBags.FirstOrDefault(b =>
            string.Equals(b.BagNumber.Trim(), barcode, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));

        if (bag is null) ShowError($"لا يوجد كيس متاح بهذا الباركود:\n{barcode}");
        else AddLine(bag);

        ScanBagNumber = string.Empty;
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void RemoveLine(InvoiceLineItem line)
    {
        Lines.Remove(line);
        RecalculateTotals();
    }

    partial void OnDiscountAmountChanged(decimal value) => RecalculateTotals();

    private void RecalculateTotals()
    {
        SubTotal = Lines.Sum(l => l.Total);
        TotalAmount = Math.Max(0, SubTotal - DiscountAmount);
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(RemainingAfterInitial));
    }

    // ─── Save ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task SaveAsync()
    {
        if (!Validate()) return;

        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            using var db = new AppDbContext();
            await using var tx = await db.Database.BeginTransactionAsync();

            var invoice = new Invoice
            {
                InvoiceNumber = InvoiceNumber,
                Date = Date,
                PaymentType = PaymentType,
                CustomerId = SelectedCustomer!.Id,
                SafeId = IsSafeVisible ? SelectedSafe!.Id : null,
                SubTotal = SubTotal,
                DiscountAmount = DiscountAmount,
                TotalAmount = TotalAmount,
                PaidAmount = PaymentType switch
                {
                    PaymentType.Cash => TotalAmount,
                    PaymentType.Partial => InitialPayment,
                    _ => 0
                }
            };

            db.Invoices.Add(invoice);
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            foreach (var line in Lines)
            {
                db.InvoiceLines.Add(new InvoiceLine
                {
                    InvoiceId = invoice.Id,
                    BloodBagId = line.BloodBagId,            // null للأصناف اليدوية
                    ProductName = line.ProductName,
                    UnitPrice = line.UnitPrice,
                    Quantity = line.Quantity,
                    Total = line.Total
                });

                // تحديث حالة الكيس فقط إذا كان مرتبطاً
                if (line.BloodBagId.HasValue)
                {
                    var bag = await db.BloodBags.FindAsync(line.BloodBagId.Value);
                    if (bag is not null) bag.Status = BloodBagStatus.Sold;
                }
            }

            var customer = await db.Customers.FindAsync(SelectedCustomer!.Id);

            switch (PaymentType)
            {
                case PaymentType.Cash:
                    var safeCash = await db.Safes.FindAsync(SelectedSafe!.Id);
                    safeCash!.Balance += TotalAmount;
                    break;

                case PaymentType.Credit:
                    customer!.Balance += TotalAmount;
                    break;

                case PaymentType.Partial:
                    customer!.Balance += (TotalAmount - InitialPayment);
                    var safePartial = await db.Safes.FindAsync(SelectedSafe!.Id);
                    safePartial!.Balance += InitialPayment;
                    break;
            }

            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            invoice.Customer = customer!;

            if (PaymentType == PaymentType.Partial)
                JournalService.PostPartialInvoice(db, invoice, InitialPayment, AccountCodes.Cash);
            else
                JournalService.PostInvoice(db, invoice,
                    PaymentType == PaymentType.Cash ? AccountCodes.Cash : AccountCodes.AccountsRec);

            await db.SaveChangesAsync();
            await tx.CommitAsync();

            ShowSuccess($"تم حفظ الفاتورة {InvoiceNumber} بنجاح ✓");
            await ResetFormAsync();
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            // ابحث عن السبب العميق للخطأ
            var message = ex.InnerException?.InnerException?.Message
                          ?? ex.InnerException?.Message
                          ?? ex.Message;

            ShowError($"حدث خطأ أثناء الحفظ:\n{message}");
        }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }

    private bool Validate()
    {
        if (SelectedCustomer is null) { ShowError("اختر العميل"); return false; }
        if (!Lines.Any()) { ShowError("أضف صنفاً واحداً على الأقل"); return false; }

        // ─── التحقق من الخصم ──────────────────────────────────
        if (DiscountAmount < 0)
        {
            ShowError("الخصم لا يمكن أن يكون قيمة سالبة"); return false;
        }
        if (DiscountAmount > SubTotal)
        {
            ShowError("الخصم لا يمكن أن يتجاوز إجمالي الفاتورة"); return false;
        }

        if (PaymentType == PaymentType.Cash && SelectedSafe is null)
        { ShowError("اختر الخزينة"); return false; }
        if (PaymentType == PaymentType.Partial)
        {
            if (SelectedSafe is null) { ShowError("اختر الخزينة"); return false; }
            if (InitialPayment <= 0) { ShowError("أدخل المبلغ المدفوع الآن"); return false; }
            if (InitialPayment >= TotalAmount)
            { ShowError("المبلغ المدفوع يجب أن يكون أقل من الإجمالي\nللدفع الكامل اختر «نقدي»"); return false; }
        }
        return true;
    }

    private async Task ResetFormAsync()
    {
        Lines.Clear();
        SelectedCustomer = null;
        DiscountAmount = 0;
        InitialPayment = 0;
        ScanBagNumber = string.Empty;
        IsQuickAddVisible = false;
        NewLineName = string.Empty;
        NewLineQty = 1;
        NewLineUnitPrice = 0;
        await LoadAsync();
    }
}

// ─── Line item DTO ────────────────────────────────────────────
public partial class InvoiceLineItem : ObservableObject
{
    /// <summary>null → صنف يدوي</summary>
    public int? BloodBagId { get; set; }
    public bool IsManual { get; set; }
    public string ProductName { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [ObservableProperty]
    [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(Total))]
    private decimal _unitPrice;

    [ObservableProperty]
    [NotifyPropertyChangedFor(nameof(Total))]
    private int _quantity = 1;

    public decimal Total => UnitPrice * Quantity;
}

// ─── Extension ────────────────────────────────────────────────
public static class BloodGroupExtensions
{
    public static string ToArabic(this BloodGroup g) => g switch
    {
        BloodGroup.APositive => "A+",
        BloodGroup.ANegative => "A-",
        BloodGroup.BPositive => "B+",
        BloodGroup.BNegative => "B-",
        BloodGroup.ABPositive => "AB+",
        BloodGroup.ABNegative => "AB-",
        BloodGroup.OPositive => "O+",
        BloodGroup.ONegative => "O-",
        _ => g.ToString()
    };
}

<file path="ViewModels/MainWindowViewModel.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Helpers;
using BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels.Base;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using System.Windows.Threading;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels;

public partial class MainWindowViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    [ObservableProperty] private ViewModelBase? _currentPage;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _currentPageTitle = "الرئيسية";
    [ObservableProperty] private string _activePage = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _backupStatus = "جاري التحقق...";

    // ─── حدث طلب الدخول لتقرير الديون ───────────────────────────────────
    public event Action? DebtReportAccessRequested;

    // ─── Navigation ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    [RelayCommand]
    private void NavigateToDashboard()
    {
        CurrentPage = new DashboardViewModel();
        CurrentPageTitle = "لوحة التحكم";
        ActivePage = "Dashboard";
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void NavigateToInvoice()
    {
        CurrentPage = new InvoiceViewModel();
        CurrentPageTitle = "فاتورة مبيعات جديدة";
        ActivePage = "Invoice";
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void NavigateToInvoiceList()
    {
        CurrentPage = new InvoiceListViewModel();
        CurrentPageTitle = "سجل الفواتير";
        ActivePage = "InvoiceList";
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void NavigateToReceipt()
    {
        CurrentPage = new ReceiptVoucherViewModel();
        CurrentPageTitle = "سند قبض جديد";
        ActivePage = "Receipt";
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void NavigateToPayment()
    {
        CurrentPage = new PaymentVoucherViewModel();
        CurrentPageTitle = "سند دفع جديد";
        ActivePage = "Payment";
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void NavigateToCustomers()
    {
        CurrentPage = new CustomersViewModel();
        CurrentPageTitle = "إدارة العملاء";
        ActivePage = "Customers";
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void NavigateToBloodInventory()
    {
        CurrentPage = new BloodInventoryViewModel();
        CurrentPageTitle = "مخزون الدم";
        ActivePage = "BloodInventory";
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// لا ينتقل مباشرة — يُطلق الحدث كي يتحقق MainWindow من الـ PIN أولاً.
    /// </summary>
    [RelayCommand]
    private void NavigateToDebtReport() => DebtReportAccessRequested?.Invoke();

    /// <summary>يُستدعى من MainWindow.xaml.cs بعد التحقق الناجح من الـ PIN.</summary>
    public void OpenDebtReport()
    {
        CurrentPage = new DebtReportViewModel();
        CurrentPageTitle = "تقرير الديون";
        ActivePage = "DebtReport";
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void NavigateToSafeReport()
    {
        CurrentPage = new SafeReportViewModel();
        CurrentPageTitle = "الخزينة";
        ActivePage = "SafeReport";
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void NavigateToOrgSettings()
    {
        CurrentPage = new OrgSettingsViewModel();
        CurrentPageTitle = "إعدادات المؤسسة";
        ActivePage = "OrgSettings";
    }

    // ─── التحقق من الـ PIN — يقرأ من DB في كل مرة ───────────────────────
    //
    // لماذا DB مباشرة وليس cache؟
    //   لو العميل غيّر الـ PIN من شاشة الإعدادات أثناء نفس الجلسة،
    //   يُطبَّق الرمز الجديد فوراً بدون إعادة تشغيل التطبيق.

    /// <summary>هل يوجد رمز سري مضبوط؟ (OwnerPinHash غير فارغ في DB)</summary>
    public async Task<bool> IsDebtReportProtectedAsync()
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var org = await db.OrgSettings.FirstOrDefaultAsync();
        return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(org?.OwnerPinHash);
    }

    /// <summary>يتحقق من صحة الرمز المُدخَل عبر PinHelper.Verify.</summary>
    public async Task<bool> VerifyDebtReportPinAsync(string pin)
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var org = await db.OrgSettings.FirstOrDefaultAsync();
        return PinHelper.Verify(pin, org?.OwnerPinHash ?? string.Empty);
    }

    // ─── Backup ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    public MainWindowViewModel()
    {
        NavigateToDashboard();
        SubscribeToBackupEvents();
    }

    private void SubscribeToBackupEvents()
    {
        BackupStatus = AutoBackupService.Instance.LastStatusText;

        AutoBackupService.Instance.BackupSucceeded += _ =>
            Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.InvokeAsync(() =>
                BackupStatus = $"✓ آخر نسخة: {DateTime.Now:hh:mm tt}");

        AutoBackupService.Instance.BackupFailed += _ =>
            Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.InvokeAsync(() =>
                BackupStatus = "⚠ فشل النسخ الاحتياطي");
    }
}

<file path="ViewModels/ReceiptVoucherViewModel.cs">
using BloodBankAccounting.Data;
using BloodBankAccounting.Helpers;
using BloodBankAccounting.Models;
using BloodBankAccounting.Printing;
using BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels.Base;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels;

public partial class ReceiptVoucherViewModel : ViewModelBase
{
    [ObservableProperty] private string _voucherNumber = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private DateTime _date = DateTime.Today;
    [ObservableProperty] private decimal _amount;
    [ObservableProperty] private string? _notes;

    [ObservableProperty] private Customer? _selectedCustomer;
    [ObservableProperty] private Safe? _selectedSafe;
    [ObservableProperty] private Invoice? _selectedInvoice;

    // ─── Customer search ─────────────────────────────────────
    [ObservableProperty] private string _customerSearchText = string.Empty;
    [ObservableProperty] private bool _isSearchDropdownOpen;

    public ObservableCollection<Customer> Customers { get; } = [];
    public ObservableCollection<Safe> Safes { get; } = [];
    public ObservableCollection<Invoice> CustomerInvoices { get; } = [];
    public ObservableCollection<Customer> FilteredCustomers { get; } = [];

    private int? _savedVoucherId;
    public bool CanPrint => _savedVoucherId.HasValue;

    public ReceiptVoucherViewModel() { _ = LoadAsync(); }

    // ─── Filter as user types ─────────────────────────────────
    partial void OnCustomerSearchTextChanged(string value)
    {
        FilteredCustomers.Clear();

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
        {
            IsSearchDropdownOpen = false;
            // Clear selection if user erased the box
            if (SelectedCustomer is not null)
            {
                SelectedCustomer = null;
                CustomerInvoices.Clear();
                Amount = 0;
            }
            return;
        }

        var lower = value.Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
        var matches = Customers
            .Where(c => c.Name.ToLowerInvariant().Contains(lower))
            .Take(12);

        foreach (var c in matches) FilteredCustomers.Add(c);
        IsSearchDropdownOpen = FilteredCustomers.Count > 0;
    }

    // ─── Pick a customer from dropdown ───────────────────────
    [RelayCommand]
    private void SelectCustomer(Customer customer)
    {
        SelectedCustomer = customer;
        CustomerSearchText = customer.Name;
        IsSearchDropdownOpen = false;
    }

    // ─── Close dropdown from View code-behind ────────────────
    public void CloseDropdown() => IsSearchDropdownOpen = false;

    private async Task LoadAsync()
    {
        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var customers = await db.Customers.Where(c => c.IsActive && c.Balance > 0)
                                          .OrderBy(c => c.Name).ToListAsync();
        var safes = await db.Safes.OrderBy(s => s.Name).ToListAsync();

        Customers.Clear(); customers.ForEach(Customers.Add);
        Safes.Clear(); safes.ForEach(Safes.Add);

        VoucherNumber = await NumberGenerator.NextVoucherNumberAsync(db);
        SelectedSafe = safes.FirstOrDefault(s => s.IsMain);
    }

    partial void OnSelectedCustomerChanged(Customer? value)
    {
        _ = LoadCustomerInvoicesAsync(value);
    }

    private async Task LoadCustomerInvoicesAsync(Customer? customer)
    {
        CustomerInvoices.Clear();
        if (customer is null) return;

        using var db = new AppDbContext();
        var invoices = await db.Invoices
            .Where(i => i.CustomerId == customer.Id
                     && (i.PaymentType == PaymentType.Credit || i.PaymentType == PaymentType.Partial)
                     && !i.IsCancelled
                     && i.PaidAmount < i.TotalAmount)
            .OrderByDescending(i => i.Date)
            .ToListAsync();

        invoices.ForEach(CustomerInvoices.Add);
        Amount = customer.Balance;
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task SaveAsync()
    {
        if (!Validate()) return;
        IsBusy = true;
        try
        {
            using var db = new AppDbContext();
            await using var tx = await db.Database.BeginTransactionAsync();

            var voucher = new ReceiptVoucher
            {
                VoucherNumber = VoucherNumber,
                Date = Date,
                SafeId = SelectedSafe!.Id,
                CustomerId = SelectedCustomer!.Id,
                RelatedInvoiceId = SelectedInvoice?.Id,
                Amount = Amount,
                Notes = Notes
            };

            db.ReceiptVouchers.Add(voucher);
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            var customer = await db.Customers.FindAsync(SelectedCustomer!.Id);
            customer!.Balance -= Amount;

            var safe = await db.Safes.FindAsync(SelectedSafe!.Id);
            safe!.Balance += Amount;

            if (SelectedInvoice is not null)
            {
                var invoice = await db.Invoices.FindAsync(SelectedInvoice.Id);
                invoice!.PaidAmount += Amount;
            }

            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            voucher.Customer = customer;
            JournalService.PostReceipt(db, voucher);
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();

            await tx.CommitAsync();
            _savedVoucherId = voucher.Id;
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CanPrint));
            ShowSuccess($"تم حفظ سند القبض {VoucherNumber} ✓");

            await LoadAsync();
            SelectedCustomer = null;
            CustomerSearchText = string.Empty;
            IsSearchDropdownOpen = false;
            Amount = 0;
        }
        catch (Exception ex) { ShowError(ex.Message); }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }

    private bool Validate()
    {
        if (SelectedCustomer is null) { ShowError("اختر العميل"); return false; }
        if (SelectedSafe is null) { ShowError("اختر الخزينة"); return false; }
        if (Amount <= 0) { ShowError("المبلغ يجب أن يكون أكبر من صفر"); return false; }
        return true;
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task PrintAsync()
    {
        if (!_savedVoucherId.HasValue) return;
        IsBusy = true;
        try { await ReceiptPrinter.PrintAsync(_savedVoucherId.Value); }
        catch (Exception ex) { ShowError(ex.Message); }
        finally { IsBusy = false; }
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Views/DashboardView.xaml">
<UserControl x:Class="BloodBankAccounting.Views.DashboardView"
             xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml/presentation"
             xmlns:x="http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml">

    <!--
        ✅ التغييرات للتجاوب:
        1. ScrollViewer Padding: "24,20,24,32" → "18,14,18,24"
           يترك مساحة أكبر للمحتوى على الشاشات الأصغر
        2. UniformGrid Columns="4": يظل 4 أعمدة — الـ ScrollViewer يتكفل بباقي التجاوب
           كل كارد يحصل على 25% من العرض المتاح تلقائياً
        3. أقسام الرسوم البيانية والمؤشرات: تستخدم "*" columns بالفعل → ممتازة التجاوب
        4. Margin داخل UniformGrid: خُفِّف قليلاً
    -->

    <ScrollViewer VerticalScrollBarVisibility="Auto"
                  Padding="18,14,18,24">
        <StackPanel>

                                               VerticalAlignment="Center" Margin="0,0,8,0"/>

                                               Foreground="White" FontSize="13"

                                </StackPanel>
                            </Border>
                            <ControlTemplate.Triggers>
                                <Trigger Property="IsMouseOver" Value="True">
                                    <Setter TargetName="lockBorder" Property="Background" Value="#2C3E6B"/>
                                </Trigger>
                            </ControlTemplate.Triggers>
                        </ControlTemplate>
                    </Button.Template>
                </Button>

<UserControl x:Class="BloodBankAccounting.Views.DebtReportView"
             xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml/presentation"
             xmlns:x="http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml">

    <!--
        ✅ التغييرات للتجاوب:
        1. الشاشة مُغلَّفة بـ ScrollViewer لضمان ظهور المحتوى على الشاشات الصغيرة
        2. Margin الخارجي: "24" → "20"
        3. بنية الأعمدة في الـ DataGrid كانت جيدة بالفعل (يستخدم * للعمود الرئيسي)
    -->

    <Grid Margin="20">
        <Grid.RowDefinitions>
            <RowDefinition Height="Auto"/>
            <RowDefinition Height="*"/>
            <RowDefinition Height="Auto"/>
        </Grid.RowDefinitions>

        <!-- Header -->
        <Border Grid.Row="0" Style="{StaticResource Card}" Margin="0,0,0,14" Padding="20">
            <StackPanel>
                <StackPanel Orientation="Horizontal" Margin="0,0,0,12">
                    <TextBlock Text="📉" FontSize="18" Margin="0,0,8,0" VerticalAlignment="Center"/>
                    <TextBlock Text="تقرير الديون المستحقة"
                               FontSize="15" FontWeight="SemiBold" Foreground="#1A2744"
                               VerticalAlignment="Center"/>
                </StackPanel>

                <StackPanel Orientation="Horizontal">
                    <TextBlock Text="إجمالي الديون: " Foreground="#7F8C8D" FontSize="14"
                               VerticalAlignment="Bottom" Margin="0,0,8,3"/>
                    <TextBlock Text="{Binding TotalDebt, StringFormat='{}{0:N2} ج.م'}"
                               Foreground="#C0392B" FontSize="24" FontWeight="Bold"
                               VerticalAlignment="Bottom"/>
                </StackPanel>
            </StackPanel>
        </Border>

        <!-- Debtors grid -->
        <Border Grid.Row="1" Style="{StaticResource Card}" Margin="0,0,0,14">
            <DataGrid Style="{StaticResource MainDataGrid}"
                      ItemsSource="{Binding Debtors}">
                <DataGrid.Columns>
                    <DataGridTextColumn Header="#" Width="40">
                        <DataGridTextColumn.Binding>
                            <Binding RelativeSource="{RelativeSource AncestorType=DataGridRow}"
                                     Converter="{StaticResource RowNumberConverter}"/>
                        </DataGridTextColumn.Binding>
                    </DataGridTextColumn>
                    <DataGridTextColumn Header="اسم العميل"
                                       Binding="{Binding Name}" Width="*"/>
                    <DataGridTextColumn Header="النوع"
                                       Binding="{Binding Type}" Width="100"/>
                    <DataGridTextColumn Header="الهاتف"
                                       Binding="{Binding Phone}" Width="130"/>
                    <DataGridTextColumn Header="إجمالي الدين (ج.م)"
                                       Binding="{Binding Balance, StringFormat='{}{0:N2}'}" Width="150"/>
                    <DataGridTextColumn Header="حد الائتمان"
                                       Binding="{Binding CreditLimit, StringFormat='{}{0:N2}'}" Width="130"/>
                </DataGrid.Columns>
                <DataGrid.RowStyle>
                    <Style TargetType="DataGridRow">
                        <Style.Triggers>
                            <DataTrigger Value="True">
                                <DataTrigger.Binding>
                                    <MultiBinding Converter="{StaticResource OverCreditLimitConverter}">
                                        <Binding Path="Balance"/>
                                        <Binding Path="CreditLimit"/>
                                    </MultiBinding>
                                </DataTrigger.Binding>
                                <Setter Property="Background" Value="#FADBD8"/>
                            </DataTrigger>
                        </Style.Triggers>
                    </Style>
                </DataGrid.RowStyle>
            </DataGrid>
        </Border>

        <!-- Legend -->
        <StackPanel Grid.Row="2" Orientation="Horizontal">
            <Border Background="#FADBD8" Width="16" Height="16" CornerRadius="3" Margin="0,0,6,0"/>
            <TextBlock Text="تجاوز حد الائتمان" Foreground="#566573" FontSize="12" VerticalAlignment="Center"/>
        </StackPanel>
    </Grid>

<file path="Views/MainWindow.xaml.cs">
// MainWindow.xaml.cs
using BloodBankAccounting.ViewModels;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Input;

namespace BloodBankAccounting.Views;

public partial class MainWindow : Window
{
    // ❌ احذف السطر ده تماماً:
    // private const string DebtReportPinHash = "03ac674216f3e15c761ee1a5e255f067953623c8b388b4459e13f978d7c846f4";

    private int _logoClickCount;
    private DateTime _firstClickTime;
    private const int RequiredClicks = 5;
    private static readonly TimeSpan ClickWindow = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(3);

    private MainWindowViewModel? _vm;

    public MainWindow()
    {
        InitializeComponent();

        if (DataContext is MainWindowViewModel vm)
        {
            _vm = vm;
            vm.DebtReportAccessRequested += OnDebtReportAccessRequested;
        }
    }

    // ─── معالجة طلب الدخول لتقرير الديون ─────────────────────────────────
    private async void OnDebtReportAccessRequested()
    {
        if (_vm is null) return;

        // ✅ لو مفيش PIN مضبوط في DB — افتح مباشرة بدون حوار
        bool isProtected = await _vm.IsDebtReportProtectedAsync();
        if (!isProtected)
        {
            _vm.OpenDebtReport();
            return;
        }

        // ✅ اطلب الـ PIN
        var pin = PinDialog.Show(this);
        if (pin is null) return;  // ألغى المستخدم

        // ✅ تحقق من DB (نفس الـ hash اللي بيستخدمه Dashboard والخزينة)
        bool valid = await _vm.VerifyDebtReportPinAsync(pin);
        if (valid)
        {
            _vm.OpenDebtReport();
        }
        else
        {
            MessageBox.Show(
                "الرمز الذي أدخلته غير صحيح.\nليس لديك صلاحية الوصول إلى تقرير الديون.",
                "وصول مرفوض",
                MessageBoxButton.OK,
                MessageBoxImage.Warning);
        }
    }

    internal void LogoBorder_MouseDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
    {
        var now = DateTime.Now;

        if (_logoClickCount > 0 && (now - _firstClickTime) > ClickWindow)
            _logoClickCount = 0;

        if (_logoClickCount == 0)
            _firstClickTime = now;

        _logoClickCount++;

        if (_logoClickCount >= RequiredClicks)
        {
            _logoClickCount = 0;
            OpenAdminPanel();
        }
    }

    private void OpenAdminPanel()
    {
        var pwdDialog = new AdminPasswordDialog();
        pwdDialog.ShowDialog();

        if (pwdDialog.Authenticated)
        {
            var panel = new SecretAdminPanel();
            panel.ShowDialog();
        }
    }
}
</file>
